The pre-trained LLM alone performed competitively, and the engine likely introduced conflicting optimization signals or implementation mismatches.

Abstract: Tax-loss harvesting demonstrates consistent benefits to long-term portfolio growth; yet implementing it efficiently often involves complex considerations that are specific to the holdings within that portfolio and the individual who owns it. We introduce a custom capital gains calculation engine and a RAG-retrieved vector store of market advisory reports to provide context for a multi-agent trade recommendation system. We investigate the effects of each context provider on the quality of recommendations, measured by relative capital gains incurred during portfolio liquidation. A 2x2 repeated-measures ANOVA revealed a significant main effect of the tax optimization engine ($F(1,29) = 9.17$, $p = .005$, $η^2_p = .240$): enabling the engine reduced tax savings by approximately 55 percentage points relative to the no-engine conditions. The RAG main effect was not significant ($p = .841$), nor was the interaction ($p = .553$). The RAG-only condition achieved the highest descriptive mean tax savings (47.7%), and the baseline condition performed second-best (30.6%), suggesting that the pre-trained language model's internalized financial knowledge may be sufficient for competent tax-loss harvesting recommendations without explicit tooling. These results indicate that augmenting LLM agents with domain-specific computation engines does not guarantee improved performance and may introduce conflicting optimization signals.

</details>


### [32] [PROOF-Gen: From Optimized Data to Better Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23911)
*Anh Ta,Junjie Zhu,Shahin Shayandeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对每个失败样本做执行轨迹反思并生成纠正性提示，从而把教师失败转为高质量示范，显著提高下游模型性能并能在部署流水线上带来增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生成并筛选策略忽略了教师失败样本中有用的近失案例，导致训练数据在每个周期重复丢失相同难例的信号；通过针对性恢复这些失败示范，可提高数据效率并减少每周期对前沿教师的浪费。

Method: 对每个失败任务：解析执行轨迹与评测反馈，使用一个‘reflector’生成纠正性指导以引导教师重新生成通过轨迹；将纠正性提示在训练前移除，使用干净的通过示范进行监督微调；评价包含通过率提升及部署后端到设备端的迁移。

Result: PROOF-Gen 通过对每个失败场景进行反思式提示优化，从教师失败的轨迹中恢复可用于训练的黄金示范。作者在 τ2-bench 上将失败场景的93%恢复为通过示范，利用扩增后的数据微调模型（Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 等）显著提升工具调用和多轮任务成功率，并在部署流水线和设备端模型上观察到正向迁移（跨语言也有提升）。

Conclusion: Per-scenario 反思式提示优化能高效回收教师失败样本的训练价值，提升工具调用代理的成功率和响应质量，且具备跨模型与跨语言迁移效果。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning on teacher-generated trajectories is the standard first stage for distilling tool-calling capabilities into deployable models. Post-training pipelines that drive shipped tool-calling agents re-run this stage on a daily or weekly cadence, paying the frontier-teacher cost each cycle, yet the mechanism is generate-and-filter (keep the teacher's passing trajectories, discard the rest) and each cycle leaves behind the same hard scenarios because failures supply no signal. On τ2-bench, 57% of teacher trials fail, two-thirds of them near-misses (most tool calls correct, undone by one decisive error).
  We introduce PROOF-Gen (Per-scenario Reflective Optimization to Overcome FailedGeneration), which recovers golden trajectories from these failures via per-scenario prompt optimization. For each failed task, a reflector analyzes the execution trace and evaluation feedback, then writes corrective guidance that steers the teacher to a passing trajectory. The guidance is stripped before training, so the student learns from clean demonstrations with no task-specific scaffold.
  On τ2-bench, per-scenario optimization recovers 93% of failed scenarios. Fine-tuned on the combined data, Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 improves from Pass^1=0.132 to 0.529 and Gemma 4 E4B-it gains +7.2pp on BFCL v4 multi-turn. In a deployed pipeline, the method lifts trajectory quality by +6.3pp goal completion and transfers to a deployed on-device model (+1.5pp goal completion; +1.7 to +5.0pp across response-quality metrics), with positive transfer in every locale (non-English average +1.48pp).

</details>


### [33] [MARS: Multi-Specialist LLM Relay System for Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23918)
*Andrei Mikhailov,Mikhail Burtsev,Alsu Sagirova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Specialist, retrieval-augmented multi-agent relay yields substantial gains on competitive programming tasks—better pass rate, lower cost and variance—by explicitly routing algorithmic reasoning through topic experts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing multi-agent pipelines use generic planner/coder/debugger roles and leave algorithmic technique selection to the backbone LLM, which underperforms on competitive programming where algorithmic insight is crucial. The authors aim to inject specialized algorithmic knowledge via topic-focused agents grounded by retrieval.

Method: Prompt-only multi-agent framework where retrieval over an algorithm-theory corpus selects a small team of topic specialists (e.g., DP, graphs). A starter agent produces an initial C++17 solution; each turn tests the candidate in a sandbox using public examples, and the active specialist decides to keep, repair, or hand off the draft, forwarding a structured packet to the next specialist. A final infrastructure-fixer pass normalizes boilerplate.

Result: On the CodeContests test split using Gemma 4, MARS achieves a pass rate of 0.624 ± 0.006 with 2.3 recorded pipeline stages per task—+14.4 percentage points over direct prompting—and approaches CodeSIM's 0.731 while using 3.3× less wall-clock cost and showing smaller variance in per-task token spend. Source code is published on GitHub.

Conclusion: MARS (Multi-Agent Relay of Specialized LLMs) improves algorithmic code generation by using retrieval-grounded, topic-specialist agents and an iterative relay pipeline, achieving higher pass rates and lower cost/variance versus direct prompting and narrowing the gap to stronger baselines.

Abstract: Large Language Models excel at code generation, yet competitive programming exposes a persistent failure mode: existing multi-agent pipelines distribute work over generic planner, coder, and debugger roles and delegate the choice of algorithmic technique to the backbone alone. We present MARS (Multi-Agent Relay of Specialized LLMs), a prompt-only framework in which each agent is a topic specialist---dynamic programming, graphs, strings, geometry, and so on---grounded by retrieval-augmented generation over an algorithm-theory corpus. Given a problem, retrieval selects a small team of relevant specialists; a starter writes an initial C++17 solution, and each subsequent turn runs the candidate against public examples in a sandbox, lets the active specialist keep, repair, or hand off the draft, and forwards a structured packet to the next specialist. A single infrastructure-fixer pass normalizes boilerplate at the end. On the CodeContests test split with Gemma 4, MARS reaches $0.624 \pm 0.006$ pass rate at $2.3$ recorded pipeline stages per task ($+14.4$ percentage points over direct prompting), closing most of the gap to CodeSIM ($0.731$) at $3.3{\times}$ lower wall-clock cost and substantially smaller variance in per-task token spend. The source code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/fckand/mars.

</details>


### [34] [Data Mixing as Mixture Experiment: Response Surface Methodology and Optimal Design for Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23922)
*Yicheng Mao,Hongru Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Reframe LLM data-mixing as a mixture-experiment problem and use sparse second-order Scheffé models plus model-robust I-optimal design to get interpretable interaction effects and more efficient proxy experiments, validated on RegMix.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Data mixing allocation is a central decision in LLM pretraining under a fixed token budget; practitioners use proxy-based small-model evaluations to choose mixtures. The paper reframes this as a classical mixture experiment to gain statistical efficiency and interpretability.

Method: Map domains to mixture components, token shares to component proportions, and proxy runs to design points; fit sparse second-order Scheffé response-surface models to validation loss over the simplex; construct model-robust I-optimal experimental designs to select proxy mixtures; validate via RegMix case study and simulation calibrated to observed proxy-training responses; compare to flexible ML predictors for prediction/ordering tasks.

Result: Developed a sparse second-order Scheffé response-surface formulation and constructed model-robust I-optimal designs for proxy data-mixing experiments. Empirically (RegMix case) the approach interprets mixture responses, finds strong relational (interaction) effects, preserves mixture rankings across model scales, competes with flexible ML predictors, and can remove ~25% of proxy runs while recovering mixture orderings.

Conclusion: LLM data mixing should be treated as an experimental-design problem; sparse Scheffé models capture additive and pairwise interaction effects, enable interpretable decomposition and more efficient proxy-run allocation, and show interactions (especially with web text) can change domain value. Model-robust I-optimal designs can substantially reduce required proxy runs without losing ordering information.

Abstract: Data mixing is a central design problem in large language model pretraining: given a fixed token budget, practitioners must decide how much data to allocate to each domain. Recent proxy-based methods address this problem by training small models on candidate mixtures, fitting a response model, and using the response to select mixtures for larger-scale training. We show that this workflow has the structure of a classical mixture experiment. Under this view, data domains are mixture components, token shares are component proportions, proxy-training runs are experimental design points, and validation loss defines a response surface over the probability simplex. We develop this formulation using sparse second-order Scheffé response-surface models and construct model-robust $\mathcal{I}$-optimal designs for proxy data-mixing experiments. Using RegMix as an empirical case study, we demonstrate how the framework can both interpret observed mixture responses and design more efficient proxy experiments. The Scheffé analysis shows that domain value is strongly relational: several domains that are weak under additive effects become favourable through pairwise interactions, especially through combinations with web-derived text. The sparse Scheffé model preserves mixture rankings across model scales and remains competitive with a flexible machine-learning predictor while providing an explicit decomposition of additive and interaction effects. In a simulation study calibrated to observed proxy-training responses, model-robust $\mathcal{I}$-optimal designs recover the relevant mixture ordering after removing about 25\% of the original proxy runs. These results suggest that LLM data mixing should be treated not only as a prediction problem, but also as an experimental-design problem in which the proxy mixtures themselves can be chosen to improve statistical efficiency.

</details>


### [35] [Evolutionary Recurrent Decision Model in Developing Adaptive and Maladaptive Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23932)
*Andrew Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ERDM是一个基于强化学习的计算框架，表明在现代-祖先环境失配与有界认知下，许多与精神病理学相关的行为可以作为适应性或次优适应的产物自然涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究进化失配（evolutionary mismatch）、有界理性（bounded rationality）和满意化决策（satisficing）如何共同导致适应性与不适应性行为，并通过可扩展的计算框架将这些机制联系到精神病理学相关现象。

Method: 构建多个重复出现的任务环境（威胁、猎物/目标、联盟），用强化学习代理（隐含有学习率、折扣因子等有界认知参数）在以生存相关指标为基础的竞争性奖励下学习；进行参数化的有效性研究（改变不利童年经历水平）以观测策略谱的涌现。

Result: 提出进化重复决策模型（ERDM），在威胁、猎物/目标追逐和联盟等反复出现的环境中，用强化学习代理和以生存指标抽象的竞争性奖励进行训练。通过改变不利童年经历的参数，模型自发产生诸如习得性无助、回避、健康人际关系和攻击性等策略，且结果与实证文献一致，展示了生态效度。

Conclusion: ERDM为理解精神病理学行为提供了一种有力且可扩展的计算工具——强调环境历史、奖励结构与认知限制的交互，但需要更严格的验证、可重复性披露和与实证数据的定量对应。

Abstract: This study introduces the evolutionarily recurrent decision model (ERDM), a computational reinforcement learning framework designed to examine how evolutionary mismatch, bounded rationality, and satisficing contribute to adaptive and maladaptive behavior. ERDM simulates agents across evolutionary recurrent environments, including threat, prey/goal-pursuits, and alliances. Agents learn through competing rewards abstracted from survival metrics. A validity study under varying adverse childhood experiences demonstrates that distinct adaptive and maladaptive strategies, such as learned helplessness, avoidance, healthy relationships, and aggression, emerge naturally without being hardwired. These results align with empirical literature, showcasing ecological validity. The results suggest that many psychopathology-relevant aspects may be interpreted as bounded cognitive systems operating under modern-ancestral environmental mismatch, positioning ERDM as a key computational cognitive tool that can be extended to other studies.

</details>


### [36] [More Rejective, Not More Discriminative: The Unit of Verification in Pre-Execution LLM Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23941)
*Yuchen Han,Cheng Yan,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: twin-prefix 提供了第一个受控、预注册的测量仪器以分离审查长度影响，结论是短审查单位（1–2 动作）在权衡捕获与误拒方面优于更长窗口；安全案例应声明审查单位并同时上报干净序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案固定“单次审查单位”（review unit），但天然轨迹中审查长度与错误类型/位置协变，导致无法孤立长度效应；仅看 catch 会被“全部拒绝”策略误导，需要只变边界并配对干净对照的受控工具。

Method: 提出 twin-prefix 框架：对每个金标准计划生成一对前缀——一个注入单个、环境可接受的错误的前缀和一个在单个写入上不同的干净孪生前缀；在五个嵌套的审查长度上判断每一对，使用预注册的 informedness（catch 减去 false rejection）评估识别能力，并通过重放被隐藏的观测追踪失败原因。

Result: 对六个审判器和两个域的实验显示：informedness 在 1–2 个动作处达到峰值；更长窗口使零样本监视器更倾向拒绝但不更具判别力；校准的短单位在对比八动作审查时可恢复高达 0.95 的informedness；没有测试到任何标签盲（label-blind）策略能稳定超越短单位。

Conclusion: Pre-execution oversight effectiveness depends critically on the chosen unit of verification; shorter units (one–two actions) maximize informedness for fallible LLM monitors, while longer review windows raise both true catches and false rejections without improving discriminative power.

Abstract: Pre-execution oversight is core to trusted monitoring in AI control: a fallible LLM monitor vets planned actions before irreversible execution. Over-blocking forfeits usefulness and pressures deployers to disable it. Every protocol must fix a unit of verification: how many actions one call reviews. Existing designs take the unit as given; its effect on fallible monitors is unmeasured. Natural traces cannot isolate it: review length co-varies with error type and position. Catch alone misleads: rejecting everything catches everything. Measuring this needs boundary variation alone and a matched clean control. We introduce the twin-prefix framework, which supplies both. Each gold plan yields a prefix with one injected, environment-accepted error and a clean twin differing in one write. Judging each pair at five nested lengths ties verdict changes to the unit alone. Discrimination is scored by pre-registered informedness, catch minus false rejection. Longer review raises catch; false rejection climbs in lockstep. Informedness peaks at one or two actions for all six judges in both domains: longer windows make zero-shot monitors more rejective, not more discriminative. Replaying withheld observations traces the failure largely to observation deprivation. Safety cases should state the unit and co-report the clean series. Our framework is the first controlled, pre-registered instrument for this choice and never reads catch alone. Our calibrated short unit recovers up to 0.95 informedness over eight-action review, and no tested label-blind policy consistently beats it.

</details>


### [37] [Recursive Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23956)
*Shengxin Zhang,Xiaomin Wu,Xiyang Wu,Jing Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在统一评估下，重复分支（BRANCH）普遍优于加深（GROW）和分解（PRUNE）；其优势部分来自探索、多路径选择，部分来自缓解截断；评估协议（配对评分）对结论影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于迭代精炼、分解、重复采样等测试期推理方法的研究常各自为政，导致不同方法之间难以横向比较。需要统一抽象与统一实验管线来公平评估这些方法的增益来源与相对效果。

Method: 提出将若干测试期推理技巧抽象为作用在“推理轨迹”上的递归算子：GROW（加深单一路径）、PRUNE（分解并重构问题）和BRANCH（采样多条备选路径并择优）。在统一的测试框架下，用相同提示、相同令牌预算和统一评分代码，将三种算子与单通道chain-of-thought基线进行比较。评测涵盖5个基准、3个前沿模型，共14个模型-基准配置、49,327条标注样本、151,876次模型调用。

Result: BRANCH在所有14个设置中均提高了准确率，平均提升5.98个百分点，是12个设置中的最优算子；GROW平均提升2.18点且在两个设置中降级；PRUNE平均仅提升0.94点。分析表明BRANCH的优势既来自探索多条推理路径，也显著归因于从截断（空输出、预算耗尽）中恢复，其增益与基线空输出率高度相关（r=0.72）。此外，非配对评估与把评分失败视为模型错误会实质性改变比较结果，甚至翻转结论，因而建议采用配对评分作为标准协议。

Conclusion: 在统一的评估框架下，反复分支（BRANCH）在大多数设置中始终优于单次链式思考基线和其余两种算子（GROW、PRUNE），平均提升明显并且在12/14设置中表现最好。将评分以配对方式执行并把评分失败单独处理会显著改变比较结论，因此应作为标准协议。

Abstract: Test-time reasoning methods such as iterative refinement, decomposition, and repeated sampling are often evaluated in isolation, making their gains difficult to compare across models, benchmarks, and evaluation pipelines. We introduce a unified view of these methods as recursion operators over an agent's reasoning trace: GROW, which deepens a single reasoning path; PRUNE, which decomposes and recomposes the problem; and BRANCH, which samples alternative reasoning paths and selects among them. We evaluate all three operators against a single-pass chain-of-thought baseline under a shared harness with identical prompts, token budgets, and grading code. Across five benchmarks and three frontier models, comprising 14 model-benchmark settings, 49,327 graded items, and 151,876 model calls, BRANCH improves accuracy in all 14 settings by an average of 5.98 percentage points and is the best-performing operator in 12. In contrast, GROW yields a mean gain of 2.18 points and degrades performance in two settings, while PRUNE improves accuracy by 0.94 points on average. Analysis shows that BRANCH's advantage arises not only from exploring multiple reasoning paths, but also from recovering from truncation: its gains strongly correlate with the baseline rate of empty, budget-exhausted outputs (r = 0.72). These results weaken the hypothesis that different problems require routing among test-time reasoning operators; at this level of abstraction, repeated branching is consistently dominant. Finally, we show that unpaired evaluation and treating scoring-pipeline failures as model errors can materially change, and even reverse, comparative conclusions, motivating paired scoring as a standard protocol for test-time-compute evaluation.

</details>


### [38] [More GPUs or a Smaller Cache? Tensor Parallelism versus KV Compression for Memory-Bound LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23962)
*Srikanta Datta Tumkur,Mehar Simhadri,Anshu Bansal,Jay Iyer,Sai Pavan Kumar,Sai Kapil Kumar,Ramesh Nampelly,Raj Dandekar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KV compression generally yields lower cost per million tokens but higher latency; tensor-parallel sharding reduces latency and enables very large models but costs more per token and is only required when model weights don't fit a single device.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Resolve practical tradeoffs between sharding KV cache via tensor parallelism (extra GPUs) and compressing/evicting KV cache on a single GPU; unify cost and latency metrics across approaches to determine which is more cost-effective under varied hardware and model sizes.

Method: Cost and performance comparison of tensor parallelism vs KV compression for LLM KV cache management

Result: Across evaluated points (Llama-2 7B & 70B; A100/A40/H100), KV compression configurations (4/8/16-bit with keep ratios to 0.25) are 1.20x–2.00x cheaper per million tokens than tensor-parallel sharding (1–8 GPUs). Compression increases per-token latency (8–93%) but multiplies capacity per dollar (up to 16.5x) vs tensor parallelism (1.21x at 8 GPUs). Tensor parallelism is necessary when model weights exceed single-GPU memory (~36B params for 80GB); otherwise compression dominates.

Conclusion: Choose KV compression for cost-efficient capacity increases when the model fits on one GPU; use tensor parallelism when model weights exceed device memory, accepting higher hardware cost for feasibility and latency benefits.

Abstract: When an LLM serving deployment runs out of KVcache room, there are two well-established ways out. Tensor parallelism shards the weights and the KV cache across two, four, or eight devices, buying memory headroom at the price of an all-reduce on every layer and a hardware bill that grows with the device count. The algorithms community shrinks the cache in place, with KV quantisation and eviction keeping a single GPU and spending a little quality instead. Compression papers report memory ratios, parallel-scaling papers report throughput curves, and almost nobody puts the two on the same cost axis. We place tensor-parallel configurations (degree 1 to 8) and KV-compressed configurations (16/8/4-bit, keep-ratios down to 0.25) on one costnormalised axis, cost per million tokens against latency, using a profiled simulator calibrated on A100, A40, and H100 hardware, and we go looking for the cost-equivalence crossover. We do not find one. Across two models (Llama-2 at 7B and 70B), three GPU types, and every level of memory relief we could construct, compression is cheaper by 1.20x to 2.00x. A 7B model on an 80 GB device cannot exhaust its KV budget within its own context window, and the boundary that decides between the strategies is model size relative to device memory, at roughly 36B parameters for an 80 GB card. Below that wall, compression dominates and extra GPUs are largely wasted spend; above it, tensor parallelism stops being a choice and becomes an entry ticket: Llama-2-70B is infeasible on one A100 at any KV setting, because the binding resource is weights, which KV compression does not touch. Tensor parallelism is the only lever that improves latency (compression makes per-token latency worse, by 8 to 93%, through batching contention), while compression is the only lever that multiplies capacity per dollar (16.5x, against 1.21x for an eightfold spend on GPUs).

</details>


### [39] [Giraffe: A Mapping Architecture from Hidden Text Representations to Visual Embeddings for Efficient Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23970)
*Nejla Ghaboosi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用单一[IMG] token和两段浅MLP（训练时有辅助模块、推理时省略）把语言模型隐藏向量映射到视觉嵌入，解决多模态设计生成时输入长度爆炸的问题，轻量且有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将文本隐藏表示转为视觉输出的方法通常用多个专用图像tokens，导致序列长度暴涨，无法高效处理包含数千token的复杂设计生成任务；因此希望用极低的token开销（每图像一个token）实现可扩展、轻量的模态桥接。

Method: 核心是两组浅MLP：每组先用压缩模块将MLLM隐藏表示降维，再通过共享的扩展模块映射到目标视觉模型嵌入（CLIP ViT‑L/14），训练时两组分别参与、使用六类损失以稳定学习、一组作为辅导器帮助主块收敛；推理仅保留主块和单个[IMG] token输出嵌入以供下游视觉模块或生成器使用。

Result: 提出了一种将MLLM隐藏token表示映射到视觉模型（如CLIP ViT‑L/14）嵌入空间的轻量级架构：每张图像仅用一个[IMG] token，使用两组浅MLP块（每组含独立压缩模块、共享扩展模块），通过六种损失函数联合训练，其中一组作为辅助只在训练时使用，推理时舍弃，从而显著降低输入长度并保持生成能力。实验在图像到设计与文本到设计任务上表现良好，且推理开销小。

Conclusion: 该方法通过把每张图像压缩为一个视觉嵌入来大幅缩短序列长度，兼顾效率与表达能力，适合需要同时处理大量文本、图像和布局信息的场景（如图形设计生成）。不过依赖视觉嵌入（如CLIP）可能在细节保真度和高分辨率生成上存在上限，训练中多损失与辅助块设计增加了优化复杂度。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in understanding and interpreting mul- timedia content. However, their ability to generate me- dia remains limited. Recent approaches have attempted to bridge this gap by translating the hidden representations of token sequences into the embedding space of visual models or directly into raw image data. However, these methods often represent each image using multiple specialised to- kens which significantly increases the input length. This be- comes a major limitation for tasks such as graphic design generation where the output typically involves a seamless blend of thousands of tokens across text, multiple images, and layout information. To address this challenge, a novel architecture is proposed that maps hidden token represen- tations to the embedding space of visual models, such as CLIP ViT-L/14, using a single [IMG] token per image. The architecture employs two shallow MLP blocks, each with a separate compression module followed by a shared expan- sion module, trained with six distinct loss functions. One block aids the other during training and is omitted during inference, resulting in a lightweight solution. Strong perfor- mance is demonstrated in both image-to-design and text-to- design generation tasks.

</details>


### [40] [EMRB: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Reasoning over Raw Electromagnetic Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24086)
*Mingxu Zhang,Ying Sun,Yuhan Li,Yang Ji,Dazhong Shen,Ke Zhang,Shan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EMRB基准，评估LLM能否通过编写、运行代码直接分析原始I/Q射频测量数据；包含200道题、5个难度、27类问题和11种信号，验证了14个LLM并提出ReconPilot流水化方法提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估当前LLM作为代码代理在处理低阶物理原始测量（非预处理特征）时的能力与局限，推动自动化射频/无线系统分析工具发展。

Method: 构建只提供原始I/Q捕获的题库（200题），要求模型先通过代码发现相关量再计算答案；使用11类信号生成带验证真值的问题集；对14个模型逐题执行并评分；提出ReconPilot分三阶段：信号侦察、针对性分析、自我校验。

Result: 14个模型得分区间24.1%–78.9%，基础测量平均84.9%，系统设计平均21.2%；ReconPilot在三个backbone上使整体分数提升3.8–17.6点，改善了13/15组合；数据与代码公开。

Conclusion: LLM在基础测量上能较好完成任务，但在系统设计与高阶推理上表现明显不足；结构化的ReconPilot能稳健提升多数模型的总体表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as code agents for scientific and engineering analysis, but their ability to analyze raw physical-layer measurements remains untested. We introduce \textbf{EMRB} (\textbf{E}lectro\textbf{m}agnetic \textbf{R}easoning \textbf{B}enchmark), which evaluates whether LLMs can analyze raw I/Q data by writing and running code. EMRB contains 200 problems across five difficulty levels and 27 question types, from signal detection to OFDM design, generated from 11 signal types with verified ground truth. Unlike benchmarks built on preprocessed features or structured tables, EMRB provides only the raw capture; the quantities each question refers to must first be discovered through code. We evaluate 14 LLMs spanning proprietary, open-weight, and reasoning-oriented families. Scores range from 24.1\% to 78.9\%, with the mean dropping from 84.9\% on basic measurement to 21.2\% on system design. We also propose \textbf{ReconPilot}, a structured method that separates signal reconnaissance, targeted analysis, and self-verification. Across three backbones, ReconPilot raises the overall score by 3.8 to 17.6 points and improves 13 of 15 backbone-level combinations tested. All data and code are publicly released in \href{https://github.com/mingxuZhang2/EMRB}{\textcolor{blue}{our GitHub repository}}.

</details>


### [41] [When Seeing Is Not Enough: Benchmarking Interactive Visual Grounding in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23978)
*Zhengxiang Wang,Owen Rambow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出受控交互式视觉指认评估，发现大型视觉-语言模型在需要主动询问以获取目标信息的场景中表现显著不足且置信度校准有问题，交互能帮助但难以替代人类的信息获取与合成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉指认通常假定存在一次性、充分的信息映射，但现实世界中的指称往往信息不全、含糊并通过交互建立。需要一个系统化评估框架来衡量模型在需要信息寻求与综合的交互式指认任务上的能力。

Method: 提出一个受控评估框架，系统化地改变预先提供的目标信息量与必须通过对话获取的信息量；在四种以人为基准的视觉语境和四种交互协议上测评多款大型视觉-语言模型，并与任务级人类基线比较；进行后续研究以验证不同描述来源（人/AI）、推理强度、重复交互、描述者与视觉上下文的稳健性。

Result: 在四种语境与四种交互协议下，现有LVLMs显著低于人类基线。交互能在后续问题用于细化或修复初始描述时带来帮助，但在无初始描述且必须通过提问获取目标信息时，模型表现最差。模型置信度普遍偏高，校准不佳。上述模式在多种条件下被重复验证。

Conclusion: 交互式视觉指认仍是一个重要且未解决的挑战。当前大型视觉-语言模型在需要通过对话主动获取目标信息的场景中表现远低于人为基线；即便能通过问答修正或细化初始描述，性能提升有限。此外，模型的置信度校准不足，经常高估自身准确率。这些结果在多种描述来源、推理条件与视觉语境中均成立。

Abstract: Visual grounding is typically evaluated as a one-shot mapping from an informative referring expression to a visual target. This formulation misses a central property of real-world reference: target information is often incomplete, ambiguous, and established through interaction. We introduce a controlled evaluation framework for interactive visual grounding in large vision-language models (LVLMs), varying how much target information is provided upfront and how much must be acquired through dialogue. Across four human-grounded visual contexts and four interaction protocols, current LVLMs perform significantly below task-level human baselines. Interaction can help when follow-up questions refine or repair an initial target description. Performance is lowest when no initial description is provided and target information must be acquired through questions, indicating that proactive question-driven grounding remains difficult. LVLMs are also poorly calibrated, often reporting confidence that exceeds their empirical accuracy. Follow-up studies confirm these patterns across varied description sources (human versus AI), reasoning efforts, repeated interactions, description providers, and visual contexts. Overall, interactive visual grounding remains an important challenge, requiring visual matching, information seeking and synthesis.

</details>


### [42] [Robust Code RL via Faulty-Code-Driven Test case Synthesis and Dense Reward Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24135)
*Yiwen Zhang,Xiaodong Yan,Zhenyu Huang,Deng Zhao,Liang Jiang,Qing Cui,Zujie Wen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种以近正确错误代码为指导的测试合成与过滤方法，并设计细粒度通过率回报来缓解合成用例的噪声，实验证明可为RL微调的代码生成模型带来实质性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在基于可验证回报的强化学习（RLVR）用于代码生成时测试用例覆盖不足导致的误报（false positive）、奖励被黑（reward hacking）和策略退化问题。

Method: (1) 收集/生成‘近正确’错误代码样本；(2) 基于这些错误样本合成测试用例以揭示潜在逻辑缺陷；(3) 训练/应用验证器代理并用行为特征聚类对合成用例进行过滤和去重；(4) 设计基于测试通过率的分步密集回报函数以缓解合成用例假阴性；(5) 用该数据集对Qwen3-32B进行RL微调并在LiveCodeBench上评估。

Result: 提出RobustTests框架：用‘近正确’错误代码驱动合成测试用例，结合行为特征聚类的验证器代理过滤无效/冗余用例，并采用基于通过率的分步密集回报函数。用该流水线增强CodeContests的测试集并用于RL微调Qwen3-32B，在LiveCodeBench上取得绝对+3%的性能提升。

Conclusion: RobustTests通过改进测试合成与过滤、并引入细粒度奖励显著提高了RLVR在代码生成任务中的稳健性和诊断能力，实验证明可带来可测的性能增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a pivotal technique for enhancing the code generation capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the efficacy of RLVR in coding implementations is fundamentally limited by the comprehensiveness of test cases, because insufficient test coverage in code validation often causes false positives, further leading to reward hacking and policy degradation. To mitigate the reward bias stemming from the suboptimal quality of current automated generation methods, we propose the RobustTests framework, which introduces a faulty-code-driven test case synthesis strategy that leverages "near correct" faulty codes to guide the model in precisely capturing latent logical discrepancies and further integrates validator agents with behavioral feature clustering to facilitate the granular filtering of invalid and redundant test cases. To address false negatives caused by inherent hallucination noise in synthetic test cases, RobustTests also incorporates a stepwise dense reward function based on pass rates, bolstering training robustness through fine-grained feedback. By employing this pipeline, we construct a high-quality dataset that augmented the test cases in CodeContests, encompassing a broader spectrum of faulty code scenarios and significantly enhances diagnostic utility. Experimental results demonstrate that, by leveraging a moderately challenging subset of problems from CodeContests for training, RL fine-tuning of Qwen3-32B via RobustTests achieves an absolute 3% performance gain on the LiveCodeBench benchmark compared to baseline methods, confirming the effectiveness of the RobustTests framework in advancing the code generation proficiency of LLMs.

</details>


### [43] [Rules Before Oracles: Auditable, User-Configurable Argument Selection for Deliberative Polling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23979)
*Muntaser Syed,Markus Zanker,Marius Silaghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并实证验证了一种可解释、参数化的名册推荐规则（one-hop reversed endorsement flow）：满足可重算性与检查性要求，在大规模模拟中表现接近不可知的标签上限；规则在顺序与捕获支持量上优于基线，能在对抗压力下保持优势；权重函数是有效的安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在审议式民意调查中，当提交内容超过选民可读量时，哪个论据被展示决定了投票结果；现实中常交由不透明的学习排序器处理，选民无法重算或质疑影响其投票的暴露机制。作者主张将可解释性作为可接受机制的条件：采用公开且可由选民参数化并基于可重算证据的规则，以便重现和争议。

Method: 形式化一个基于两极化理由集合（bipolar justification sets）的投票框架；定义评价名册（slate）的三项指标：理由覆盖（coverage）、到达顺序（order）和捕获的支持质量/质量总量（captured endorsement mass）；提出满足七个可检验准则的可解释公民推荐器规则：一跳反向背书流（one-hop reversed endorsement flow），由关系权重函数参数化；实现一个主体式（agentic）模拟器，记录每次投票的所有名册，在约17000次种子配对运行中评估规则与不透明排序器和随机基线的表现；考察对抗性压力和不同权重策略（平权、作者计数归一化等）下的鲁棒性。

Result: 提出的规则满足七项可检验标准；在模拟中，所提供的名册在总体标签阅读上限（label-reading ceiling）下仅差0.035，表明任何不受约束的排序器的优势被限制且很小；仅就覆盖性而言（在非退化创作中），该规则与随机名册无显著差异（因为工具对顺序和善意盲视）；但在顺序和捕获支持量两项上，该规则在每个前缀上领先，且在对抗压力增大时领先幅度扩大，在支持量上领先因子为3.3；当存在大量无理由提交时，覆盖劣势回归并扩大；标签同质性刷票在平坦权重策略下将完整性从0.81降至0.34，在作者计数归一化下回升至0.44，表明权重函数是可带来约10%完整性改善的安全控制。作者将覆盖与支持量之间的抉择刻画为一条前沿，强调只有可解释规则才能将该抉择交还给受影响者，并将其映射至开源点对点平台的实现。

Conclusion: 可解释且公开可重算的排序规则在实践中可行：它们能以有界且较小的劣势替代不透明排序器，同时提供可控的覆盖—支持量权衡和针对刷票的防护策略；将该规则作为开源点对点平台的一部分，可增强审议投票的问责性与可控性。

Abstract: In a deliberative poll, once submissions outnumber what anyone will read, some mechanism chooses which arguments each voter sees, acquiring much of the decision; practice delegates it to opaque learned rankers, so a voter cannot recompute or contest the exposure that shaped their vote. We ask whether it can be a published rule over publicly recomputable evidence with parameters held by the voter, treating legibility as an admissibility condition on usable mechanisms, not an objective traded against accuracy. We formalise a poll over bipolar justification sets, judging a slate by reason coverage, the order it arrives in, and captured endorsement mass; we give seven checkable criteria for a civic recommender and a rule meeting them: a one-hop reversed endorsement flow parameterised by a relation-weight function. An agentic simulator records every slate at every vote, over about 17,000 seed-paired runs. Served slates fall 0.035 short of a label-reading ceiling upper-bounding every selection procedure, opaque ones included: any unconstrained ranker's advantage is bounded and small. On coverage alone, with non-degenerate authoring, the rule is indistinguishable from a random slate, a null due to an order-blind, charity-blind instrument; on the other two it leads at every prefix by a margin widening with adversarial pressure and dominates on mass by a factor of 3.3. Once a realistic fraction of submissions carries no reasons, the coverage margin returns and grows. Label-homogeneous flooding collapses completeness from 0.81 to 0.34 under a flat weight policy, only to 0.44 under author-count normalisation, making the weight function a security control worth 10% of completeness. The choice between ranking arms is a position on a coverage-versus-mass frontier, not a fact, the kind of choice only a legible rule can hand to the person it affects. It maps onto an open-source peer-to-peer platform.

</details>


### [44] [Paritok-4B: Intent-Conditioned Context Compression for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24188)
*Jiayu Shi,Luzhuo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Paritok-4B：一种抽取式、意图条件化的 4B LoRA 压缩器，专为编码代理设计，在保留关键标识符的同时把上下文压缩到约 25–28%，以可自托管的低成本方式在大多数案例下保留绝大部分求解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用提示压缩器训练于散文数据，往往会改写标识符或丢失代码中对代理至关重要的精确片段，导致编码代理在多轮交互中支付高昂的上下文 token 费用并损失功能。需要一种保留精确标识符和路径、按意图选择保留行的压缩器。

Method: 使用 gpt-4.1-mini 作为教师，在 67,074 条真实 OpenHands 轨迹上蒸馏并筛选得 40,606 条验证示例，采用 Qwen3-4B 作为基础模型并以 LoRA 方式微调得到 4B 适配器。模型采取“抽取式”选择输入片段而非重写，并在压缩时接受当前子任务的意图条件。评测包含 300 个 SWE-bench Lite 实例和真实代理产出的带行号输入。

Result: 在 300 个 SWE-bench Lite 实例上，Paritok-4B 将上下文压缩到 25.7%（gpt-4.1-mini 为 50.2%，gpt-5 为 61.9%），保留 86.5% 的未压缩单次求解质量；对真实代理的 cat -n 风格输入压缩到 27.8%，保留 89.3% 的求解质量。模型输出中 96.0% 的标识符/路径/数字出现在输入中（持有于验证集为 96.2%）。保留行在意图相关性上高出被移除行 +0.067（95% CI [+0.056, +0.078]）。McNemar 检验（paired）在真实代理样本上 p=0.079，表明在该样本量下压缩到约四分之一并未显著降低求解率。模型为 264MB 适配器，可在单张 24GB GPU 本地部署，从经济角度 gpt-5 作为压缩器反而成本高于其带来的 token 节省。权重、数据与评测脚本以 Apache 2.0 开源。

Conclusion: Paritok-4B 是一款面向编码代理轨迹的抽取式、意图条件化的 4B LoRA 压缩器，能把代理上下文压缩到约原始大小的四分之一同时大部分保留单次求解质量，并在成本上优于使用大模型（如 gpt-5）作为压缩器。

Abstract: Coding agents re-send large file reads and tool outputs to a frontier LLM every turn, and this context dominates their token bill. General-purpose prompt compressors are trained on prose and suit code poorly: they paraphrase identifiers and drop the exact spans an agent needs to edit. We present Paritok-4B, a 4B LoRA compressor for coding-agent trajectories built on two commitments. It is extractive: it selects spans rather than rewriting them, and 96.0% of the identifiers, paths, and numbers it emits already appear in its input, holding at 96.2% on held-out SWE-bench Lite output. It is intent-conditioned: told the agent's current task, it acts chiefly inside a retained segment, selecting which lines survive (retained lines are +0.067 more intent-relevant than removed ones, paired 95% CI [+0.056, +0.078]) rather than changing how much is retained. We distil a gpt-4.1-mini teacher over 67,074 real OpenHands trajectories into 40,606 validated examples and fine-tune Qwen3-4B. On all 300 SWE-bench Lite instances, Paritok-4B compresses agent context to 25.7% of its size, 2.0x harder than a gpt-4.1-mini compressor (50.2%) and 2.4x harder than gpt-5 (61.9%), while retaining 86.5% of uncompressed single-shot solve quality. Fed the cat -n line-numbered input real agents produce, it compresses slightly less (27.8%) and retains more (89.3%); there the paired test is informative, with 30 instances solved only uncompressed and 17 only compressed, an exact McNemar p=0.079, so at this sample size compressing context to roughly a quarter of its size does not significantly reduce the solve rate. The model is a 264 MB adapter that self-hosts on one 24 GB GPU with no per-token compressor fee, which at list prices decides the economics: gpt-5 as a compressor is net-negative, costing more than the downstream tokens it saves. Weights, data, and evaluation scripts are open (Apache 2.0).

</details>


### [45] [Memory Is Not Always Needed: Characterizing Conditional Memory in Scientific Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23982)
*Zhen Bi,Xueshu Chen,Yan Wang,Zhizhi Peng,Haosen Hong,Zhen Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Bingyu Zhu,Jungang Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Selective, input-aware memory routing improves reliability: route conditional memory only when and where it helps, using input proxies to predict memory utility, outperforming static or random routing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: They want to understand how conditional memory affects scientific reasoning in language models and when it should be used to improve retrieval and computation without harming performance.

Method: They analyze memory impact via knowledge-boundary characterization and targeted interventions at memory-enabled knowledge-circuit nodes, then train a router that uses pre-generation task-specific input proxies to predict memory activation, injection location, and contribution strength, evaluating on biological and chemical benchmarks across models and task types.

Result: They propose a Knowledge Boundary-Aware Router that activates memory selectively based on task-specific input proxies, deciding whether to use memory, where to inject it, and its strength. This approach reduces harmful memory effects and better preserves helpful contributions across biological and chemical reasoning benchmarks.

Conclusion: Selective memory allocation guided by knowledge boundary-aware routing yields more reliable scientific reasoning, avoiding regressions from indiscriminate memory use and preserving beneficial contributions across tasks and models.

Abstract: Scientific reasoning requires language models to retrieve specialized knowledge and incorporate it reliably into multi-step computation. Conditional memory provides an explicit lookup pathway that complements dense neural representations, but its usefulness is inherently input- and computation-dependent: retrieved information may repair missing scientific associations, yet it may also introduce distracting shortcuts or interfere with reasoning that the base model can already perform correctly. In this work, we systematically investigate when, where, and to what extent conditional memory should participate in scientific reasoning. We characterize the scientific knowledge boundary and controlled interventions on memory-enabled knowledge-circuit nodes. Based on these analyses, we propose a Knowledge Boundary-Aware Router that uses task-specific input proxies available before generation to determine whether memory is activated, which layer-stage nodes receive memory signals, and how strongly these signals contribute. Experiments on biological and chemical reasoning benchmarks, covering two backbone families and six task types, show that memory effects vary substantially across inputs, tasks, and injection locations. Compared with static and activation-rate-matched random routing, our approach more consistently preserves beneficial memory contributions while suppressing memory-induced regressions, establishing selective memory allocation as an important principle for reliable scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [46] [SA-Bench: Evaluating Semantic Alignment in LLM-Based Paper Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24252)
*Xue Hu,Zewei Pan,Zeli Su,Zhou Liu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用于量化 LLM 复现代码语义漂移的 SA-Bench（30 篇论文、1,491 个 SAUs、360 次评估），结果显示现有 LLM+scaffold 组合在语义忠实度上表现很差（平均 0.221），主要失败源于实现错误与占位，提示未来应侧重语义级规范验证的 scaffolds 与评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理能生成可执行复现代码，但常常在不显眼的地方偏离论文规范（语义漂移），这会误导研究复现与下游研究。需要一个细粒度、可量化的诊断基准来衡量并定位语义失真，以推动更可靠的生成策略与工具。

Method: 作者挑选 30 篇 ICLR/ICML/NeurIPS 2025 论文，人工将每篇论文的实现规范分解为原子且可验证的实现断言（SAUs），覆盖 5 个 ML 领域与四类漂移维度（数值、方法学、协议、顺序）。为 4 个 LLM 模型 × 3 种 scaffold 配置生成代码并运行评估，对 1,491 个 SAU 进行标注与打分，汇总得分并分析失败模式与 scaffolds 效果。

Result: SA-Bench 提供了一个诊断性基准，覆盖 30 篇 2025 年顶会论文，构建 1,491 个可验证的语义对齐单元（SAUs），按数值、方法学、协议与顺序四个漂移维度评估 LLM 生成的复现代码。对 12 种生成器配置（4 模型 × 3 scaffolds）共 360 次评估，最强配置（Claude+PaperCoder）平均 SAU 得分 0.301，总体均值 0.221。主要失败模式为实现不匹配与占位（stubs），说明代理通常尝试满足需求但实现不正确；优化可执行性的 scaffolds 对科学复现帮助有限，需发展以语义规范验证为核心的 scaffolds。基准、注释与评估流水线已公开。

Conclusion: LLM 生成的复现代码在语义层面容易失真；衡量这种失真需要细粒度、可验证的断言（SAUs），并且单靠增强可执行性的 scaffolds 不足以提高科学忠实度。要显著改进，需要：扩展语义验证的 scaffolds、提高注释与测试的覆盖、并将语义一致性作为核心优化目标。

Abstract: LLM agents can generate paper reproduction code, yet often produce scientifically unfaithful implementations. We define this failure mode as semantic drift, where generated code silently diverges from the paper's specifications. We introduce SemanticAlign-Bench(SA-Bench), a diagnostic benchmark covering 30 papers from ICLR, ICML and NeurIPS 2025. For each paper, we decompose its specifications into atomic and verifiable implementation claims, which we call Semantic Alignment Units (SAUs) and evaluate repositories along four diagnostic dimensions spanning numerical, methodological, protocol and ordering drift. In total, we construct 1,491 SAUs across five ML domains and evaluate 12 generator configurations (4 models $\times$ 3 scaffolds). Even the strongest configuration (Claude+PaperCoder) achieves a mean SAU score of only 0.301 out of 1.0, with an overall mean of 0.221 across 360 evaluations. A failure taxonomy reveals that agents attempt most requirements but implement them incorrectly, with implementation mismatch and stubs accounting for the majority of zero-scored claims. Our analysis further indicates that scaffolds optimized for executability provide limited leverage for scientific reproduction; narrowing the gap requires scaffolds that prioritize semantic specification verification. The benchmark, annotations and evaluation pipeline are publicly available.

</details>


### [47] [Diverse by Reasoning: Harnessing the Wisdom of LLM Crowds for Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24001)
*Nirupam Chetlapalli,Yiming Liao,Min-Chun Chen,Keke Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对模型推理轨迹建模并按行为聚类，挑选代表性模型可以用远小于全体规模的队伍获得更好或相当的预测效果，从而大幅降低调用次数与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用多模型群体（LLM crowds）进行未来预测，利用‘众智’提升预测性能，但增加模型数量不一定带来行为多样性，存在冗余；因此提出基于行为感知的构建方法，通过模型推理轨迹刻画并聚类以选取代表性成员。

Method: 对25个LLM在七个独立开发任务上收集推理轨迹（reasoning traces），定义行为相似度度量，对模型进行聚类（采用K-means++），在每个簇中选取中位模型作为代表，构成小规模众包并在两个未来预测基准上与全体投票等基线比较；报告性能、调用次数与成本节约。

Result: 在25个LLM、7个开发基准和2个预测基准上实证：基于K-means++对推理行为聚类并选取三模型中位代表的队伍（medoid crowd）在两个预测基准上均优于对25模型的常规投票，同时将模型调用减少88%、推理成本约降低80%。结果表明队伍构成比规模更关键，且代表性行为多样性比简单最大化多样性更有效。

Conclusion: 行为感知的代表性选取是一种高效构建LLM众包预测队伍的策略：它能在保持或提升预测性能的同时显著降低成本，强调了‘谁组成队伍’比‘队伍有多大’更重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for future prediction, motivating the use of multiple models as a wisdom-of-the-crowd mechanism. However, simply increasing crowd size does not guarantee effective diversity, as different LLMs may exhibit redundant behaviors. We propose a behavior-aware framework for constructing diverse LLM crowds. The framework characterizes models using their reasoning traces on independent development tasks, clusters models by behavioral similarity, and selects representatives for collective prediction. We evaluate 25 LLMs using seven development benchmarks for behavioral diversity modeling and two future-prediction benchmarks for evaluating diverse crowds' performance. Our results show that crowd composition can matter more than crowd size: a three-model medoid crowd based on K-means++ behavioral clustering outperforms conventional voting over all 25 models on both prediction benchmarks, while reducing model calls by 88% and inference cost by approximately 80%. The results further suggest that representative behavioral diversity, rather than simply maximizing diversity, is important for constructing effective LLM crowds

</details>


### [48] [ReproAgent: Contract-Guided Paper-to-Code Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24291)
*Xue Hu,Zewei Pan,Zhongyuan Wang,Zhou Liu,Zeli Su,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReproAgent 用实现契约 + 两条信息通道（实现需求、参考证据），通过四阶段流水线系统化地将论文转为可执行仓库，在 PaperBench Code-Dev 上显著优于同类骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文到代码的复现困难在于显式规范（算法、指标、制品）在长期代理轨迹中丢失，而隐式细节（框架默认、继承约定）未在论文中给出。需要一种能同时保留显式信息并补全隐式信息的系统化方法。

Method: 提出 ReproAgent——一个四阶段的 Prepare–Plan–Generate–Repair 流水线，围绕一个持久的实现契约展开。实现契约通过两条通道具体化：实现需求通道（将论文片段转为代码义务）与参考证据通道（从相关仓库检索内容与结构证据）。两条通道以工作包为单位绑定，并投射到文件级别契约，供生成与修复阶段使用。

Result: 在 PaperBench Code-Dev 基准上，ReproAgent 在相同 backbone 的 scaffold 下取得了最高平均分（在 Claude-Sonnet-4.5 和 Gemini-3-Flash 上均成立）。端到端通道消融实验与逐篇案例分析支持两条通道的贡献。代码与实验制品公开。

Conclusion: 该方法有效结合显式论文内容与外部结构化证据，提升论文到代码的复现质量；但仍需更详细的消融/错误分析、通道实现细节与跨域泛化测试以验证稳健性。

Abstract: Paper-to-code reproduction asks scientific AI agents to turn research papers into executable repositories that preserve the paper's method, protocol and artifacts. This is difficult because the specification is split: explicit paper content such as algorithms, metrics and artifacts is often lost across long agent trajectories, while implicit details such as framework defaults and conventions inherited from related work are absent from the paper. We introduce ReproAgent, a four-stage Prepare--Plan--Generate--Repair pipeline built around a persistent implementation contract with two channels: an implementation-requirement channel that turns paper snippets into code obligations, and a reference-evidence channel that retrieves content and structure evidence from related repositories. Both are bound to work packages, projected into file-level contracts, and consumed across generation and repair. On PaperBench Code-Dev, ReproAgent reaches the highest mean score among same-backbone scaffolds under both Claude-Sonnet-4.5 and Gemini-3-Flash. End-to-end channel ablations and per-paper cases support the contribution of both channels. Code and experimental artifacts are publicly available.

</details>


### [49] [Incorporating Cognitive Load and Knowledge Transfer for Multi-Domain Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24005)
*Haotian Zhang,Shucun Wang,Jinze Wu,Liang Ding,Shuochen Liu,Zhenya Huang,Jing Sha,Shijin Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LT-MKT通过利用LLM增强的多域分层图、显式认知负荷建模与知识迁移模块，联合刻画多域学习中的关键因素，从而在多数据集上实现了SOTA的知识追踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实学习场景多域并存，存在认知负荷（跨域在时间与知识维度上的管理成本）与知识迁移（域间知识互相影响），传统单域KT方法无法充分刻画这两类因素，因而需要新的模型进行准确状态估计。

Method: 构建包含题目与概念文本信息的多域分层图（利用大语言模型生成表达），显式建模跨域的时序与知识维度特征以刻画认知负荷，并设计知识迁移模块模拟域内与域间知识状态传播，联合训练用于预测学生作答表现。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验显示，LT-MKT在预测准确性上优于现有方法，达到或接近当前基线的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 本文提出LT-MKT方法，通过联合建模认知负荷和知识迁移改善多域知识追踪，实现了对学生未来表现的更准确预测。

Abstract: Knowledge Tracing (KT) aims to assess students' dynamic knowledge states from their learning histories. While most existing KT methods focus on single-domain learning with notable success, real-world learning scenarios often involve multiple domains simultaneously, introducing two critical factors: 1) Cognitive load, arising from managing learning across domains in both temporal and knowledge dimensions. 2) Knowledge transfer, where knowledge states in one domain influence related states both within and across domains. In this paper, we focus on exploring these factors to improve students' knowledge state assessment in multi-domain learning scenarios and propose a novel method incorporating cognitive Load and knowledge Transfer for Multi-domain Knowledge Tracing (LT-MKT). Specifically, to bridge isolated domains, LT-MKT first integrates textual information from questions and their associated concepts to construct a Multi-domain Hierarchical Graph, leveraging the advanced representational capabilities of large language models (LLMs). Then, cross-domain features in both the temporal and knowledge dimensions are explicitly modeled to capture the effects of cognitive load. Additionally, a knowledge transfer module is designed to model the propagation of knowledge states within and across domains. By jointly modeling these factors, LT-MKT enables more accurate prediction of students' future performance. Finally, extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [50] [From State to Action: OODA-Tool for Reliable Multi-Turn Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24368)
*Rongfeng Guo,Yinxuan Huang,Yusen Wu,Maoqing Zhong,Yunlu Chen,Meng Tang,Teng Long,Vincent Tao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OODA-Tool，用OODA循环把状态跟踪与动作生成解耦，通过中间受控状态提升多回合工具使用成功率，尤其在小模型及需累积信息的任务上效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归策略中状态跟踪与动作生成耦合导致‘状态-动作竞争’，可能覆盖或忽视早期交互信息，需一种架构化闭环策略以保护累积状态并保证动作与当前任务状态一致。

Method: 将生成过程分为Observe（重构任务状态）、Orient（判定是否执行）、Decide（形成可接受动作结构）和Act（实现外部输出）四阶段，使用类型化中间状态与控制器校验代替直接从历史生成动作，评估对象为Qwen3系列0.6B–14B并进行消融与迁移试验。

Result: OODA-Tool improves task success across model sizes, especially for smaller models and tasks requiring cross-turn information; it separates state preservation and action realization via Observe-Orient-Decide-Act stages with controller-checked intermediate states.

Conclusion: 封闭环策略和分阶段检查能缓解状态-动作竞争，从而稳定提升多回合多工具任务的执行成功率，具有鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Reliable multi-turn tool use requires an agent to preserve an evolving task state and ensure that each action remains consistent with it. However, direct function-calling and ReAct-style policies learn state tracking and action generation within the same autoregressive trajectory. This coupling creates state-action competition: the pressure to produce the next call can overwrite or ignore information accumulated earlier in the interaction. Inspired by Boyd's Observe-Orient-Decide-Act cycle, we introduce OODA-Tool, a typed closed-loop policy designed to mitigate this competition by separating state preservation from action realization. Rather than generating an action directly from the interaction history, OODA-Tool routes each decision through controller-checked intermediate states, ensuring that the final output remains grounded in the current task state. Specifically, Observe reconstructs the task state, Orient determines whether execution is warranted, Decide forms an admissible action structure, and Act realizes the external output. We evaluate OODA-Tool against direct function-calling and ReAct policies using Qwen3 models ranging from 0.6B to 14B across multi-turn, multi-tool, and incomplete-information settings. OODA-Tool consistently improves task success across model sizes, with larger gains on smaller models and on tasks whose actions depend strongly on information accumulated across turns and prior tool results. Controlled variants, stage-level ablations, and transfer evaluations further demonstrate the robustness of these improvements.

</details>


### [51] [Reflection with Action-Induced Visual Differences for Desktop GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24015)
*Yijie Ma,Chaoyue Niu,Fan Wu,Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决桌面GUI中变化隐蔽、证据不足的问题，提出以证据优先的两阶段反思器（EFR）：先显式提取动作相关视觉差异，再基于清洗后证据验证结果，从而提高反思准确率和端到端任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面GUI界面通常界面复杂且变化分散，传统反思器需在单步内完成变化检测与结果验证，证据隐含且推理基础薄弱，导致反思准确性不足，从而影响整个POR流程的决策可靠性。

Method: 在POR框架下，引入两阶段反思器：第一阶段检测并用Set-of-Marks标注动作位置与候选变化区域，提取显著的视觉差异；第二阶段对这些候选差异进行描述、过滤以保留与动作相关的证据，并基于清洗后的证据完成最终结果验证与判定。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified与WindowsAgentArena两个基准上，EFR使反思器准确率提升约7.11%，并分别带来平均端到端成功率提高5.94%与4.95%。此外，EFR通过降低视觉搜索复杂度和推理负担，提高了反思的可解释性与稳健性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Evidence-First Reflection (EFR)通过将反思过程分为“提取证据（视觉差异）”与“基于证据的结果校验”两步，显著提升了反思器在桌面GUI环境中对操作后状态变化的识别与判定能力，从而带来更强的端到端任务成功率。

Abstract: The Planner-Operator-Reflector (POR) framework is widely used in GUI agents to maintain objective alignment in complex tasks through modular collaboration. However, desktop GUIs introduce a key challenge: large, dense interfaces often exhibit subtle or scattered state changes, placing most of the burden on the reflector, which must compare pre- and post-action screens, while the planner and operator reason over a single state. Existing reflectors collapse change detection and outcome verification into one step, leaving evidence implicit and yielding weakly grounded decisions. To address this limitation, we propose Evidence-First Reflection (EFR), a two-stage reflector that explicitly decouples action-induced visual differences extraction from outcome verification. EFR identifies the action location and candidate changed regions with Set-of-Marks annotations, describes and filters action-relevant changes, and makes the final judgment from the cleaned evidence. This evidence-reasoning decoupled design makes reflection better grounded in screen transitions, while reducing both visual search complexity and reasoning burden. Experiments on OSWorld-Verified and WindowsAgentArena demonstrate that EFR improves reflector accuracy by 7.11%, yielding average end-to-end task success gains of 5.94% and 4.95% on the two benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [52] [PeakBench: Benchmarking Resource-Aware Tool Invocation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24509)
*Zhi-Kai Chen,Xu-Xiang Zhong,Song-Yan Li,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PeakBench，一个包含依赖注释与资源剖面的可执行多工具工作流基准，并用分离逻辑规划与物理调度的评估框架揭示并诊断资源相关失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based agents increasingly coordinate multiple tools and need to execute tool calls in parallel for low latency; however, safe parallel execution under resource constraints is hard and underexplored by existing benchmarks, which focus on selection and arguments under serial execution, leaving a scheduling/resource dimension untested and causing practical failure modes (slow safe serial vs fast but overflow-prone parallel).

Method: 构建可执行工作流集合，标注执行依赖并测量各步骤的资源消耗；设计两部分评估框架（逻辑规划评估与物理调度评估）与一组专用指标，用以解耦错误归因；在该环境下评估现有代理行为，比较有无资源信息暴露对调度效果的影响。

Result: The paper introduces PeakBench, a benchmark of executable multi-tool workflows with dependency annotations and measured resource profiles, plus a two-part evaluation framework that separates logical planning from physical scheduling with dedicated metrics. Experiments show that good logical plans do not guarantee safe/efficient execution under constrained resources, and that providing resource information reduces avoidable overflows and improves utilization.

Conclusion: Resource-aware scheduling is necessary for safe, efficient LLM-agent tool orchestration; benchmarks must include scheduling/resource dimensions. PeakBench serves as a diagnostic testbed showing that exposing resource profiles helps reduce overflows and improve utilization, but strong planning alone is insufficient.

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly solve tasks by invoking multiple tools, where parallel execution is essential for low latency but difficult to manage safely. Existing agent benchmarks primarily evaluate tool selection, argument generation, and end-to-end success under mostly serial execution, largely overlooking valid parallelization and resource-constrained scheduling. This missing scheduling dimension creates a practical failure mode: serial execution is safe but slow, while resource-agnostic parallel execution is fast but prone to avoidable resource overflows. To address this gap, we introduce PeakBench, a benchmark of executable multi-tool workflows with execution-grounded dependency annotations and measured resource profiles. A central challenge in evaluating such workflows is attribution: failures and inefficiencies may arise from incorrect dependency planning, poor resource-constrained scheduling, or both. PeakBench addresses this challenge with a two-part evaluation framework that disentangles logical planning from physical scheduling, with dedicated metrics for each dimension. Using this framework, we show that strong logical planning does not reliably translate into safe or efficient execution under resource constraints. We further show that exposing resource information can reduce avoidable overflows and improve resource utilization, making PeakBench a useful testbed for diagnosing resource-aware agent behavior. Code is available at https://github.com/Czzzk/Staggering-the-Peaks.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Confidence: Test-Time Scaling for Multi-Turn Search Agents via Retrieval Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24024)
*Hyunho Kook,Junhyuk So,Tianyu Fu,Haizhong Zheng,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在检索增强的多回合代理环境下，传统基于置信度的投票受“copy inflation”影响失效。RGV用答案与检索文档的词汇重叠度为回合评分，在上下文污染外计算信号，从而稳健提升聚合效果且不需额外LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM作为多回合检索代理常态化，需要一种在检索文档被并入上下文后仍能可靠对多次rollout结果进行聚合的机制，传统基于内部置信度的加权在此场景下表现不佳，因此需寻找对检索-复制偏差不敏感的评分信号。

Method: 识别问题：当检索文档被追加到上下文时，来自文档的被复制token会获得系统性较高的log-prob，导致置信度值在同一问题内趋于平坦（copy inflation），削弱加权投票效果。提出RGV：对每次rollout计算其最终答案与该rollout检索文档之间的词汇重叠分数（例如精确匹配或覆盖率），以该重叠分数作为权重进行投票。该分数在上下文外计算，避免使用被污染的token log-prob且无需额外LLM调用。通过四个检索-代理基准与五种LLM验证效果并进行少数正确样本的特别分析。

Result: RGV (Retrieval-Grounded Voting) reliably improves aggregation for multi-turn retrieval-augmented LLM agents by scoring rollouts via lexical overlap between the rollout’s final answer and the documents it retrieved, avoiding contaminated token log-probabilities caused by copied context. Empirically yields up to +5.4% overall accuracy and up to +35% improvement on minority-correct questions across four benchmarks and five LLMs.

Conclusion: RGV是一种简单、高效且可复制的替代加权投票方法，能缓解因检索内容加入上下文而导致的对数概率膨胀问题，在多个基准和模型上均显著优于基于log-prob的置信度投票。

Abstract: Confidence-based voting aggregates parallel LLM rollouts by weighting each with internal signals such as token log probabilities, and has been actively studied for single-turn reasoning. However, modern LLMs increasingly act as multi-turn search agents that retrieve and condition on external documents. In this paper, we show that confidence-based voting transfers poorly to this multi-turn setting, and identify the underlying failure reason as copy inflation: when retrieved documents are appended to an agent's context, tokens copied from those documents receive systematically inflated log probabilities. This flattens confidence scores within each question and weakens the resulting weighted vote. To address this issue, we propose Retrieval-Grounded Voting (RGV), which scores each rollout by the lexical overlap between its final answer and the documents it retrieved. By computing the signal outside the contaminated context, RGV sidesteps both token log probabilities and additional LLM calls. Across four search-agent benchmarks and five LLMs, RGV consistently outperforms confidence-based voting, with gains of up to +5.4% accuracy and +35% on minority-correct questions, where the correct answer appears in only 1-2 of 8 rollouts.

</details>


### [54] [Joint Optimization of Tool Creation and Use for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24571)
*Zhi Rui Tam,Chieh-Yen Lin,Yun-Nung Chen,Shao-Hua Sun,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMITH uses RL to jointly train a model to write and use tools, with multi-axis rewards, enabling a 4B model to generate usable tools and outperform larger untrained tool-writers on procedural reasoning and out-of-domain tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing tool-creation approaches prompt frozen LLMs at inference, causing a mismatch between the model that writes tools and the model that uses them; SMITH aims to close this loop so generated schemas are usable by the same policy.

Method: SMITH jointly trains tool creation and tool use within a single policy via reinforcement learning. Rollouts are either build tasks (write a tool from few examples) or use tasks (invoke a pooled tool on a held-out question). Three distinct reward axes (schema, code, outcome) provide separate gradients for different failure modes.

Result: A 4B Qwen3 trained with SMITH on 13 procedural reasoning tasks achieved 79.8% macro-average accuracy on held-out tasks, outperforming evaluated baselines including an untrained 30B-A3B tool-writer. It also improved on TabMWP-Hard (40.4) and out-of-domain GQA (42.6, +7.6 over the best same-backbone inference-time baseline) without visual/tabular training data; tools produced by 4B models improved other models' performance.

Conclusion: Jointly training tool writing and usage with separate reward signals is effective: small models can learn to generate practical tools that improve reasoning and transfer to other models. However, more details on datasets, training stability, ablations, scalability, and reproducibility are needed to fully assess generality and practical deployment.

Abstract: Tool-augmented language models are bounded by the APIs humans bothered to write; existing tool-creation systems patch this by prompting a frozen LLM at inference time, leaving the model that writes a tool decoupled from the one that uses it, with no signal that the schemas it produces are schemas it can invoke. We propose SMITH (Schema-grounded Multi-task Iterative Tool Honing), a reinforcement learning framework that jointly trains tool creation and tool use inside a single policy. Each rollout is either a build task (write a tool from a few examples) or a use task (invoke a pooled tool on a held-out question). Three separate reward axes catch schema, code, and outcome failures independently, so each failure mode contributes its own gradient. A 4B Qwen3 trained with SMITH on 13 procedural reasoning tasks with exact verifiers reaches 79.8 macro-average accuracy on held-out tasks, the best across all evaluated methods and ahead of an untrained 30B-A3B tool-writer. It also reaches 40.4 on TabMWP-Hard and 42.6 on out-of-domain GQA (+7.6 over the best same-backbone inference-time baseline), without any visual or tabular training data. Tools written by our 4B models also lifted the performance of LFM-2.5-350M and Qwen3-30B-A3B under same reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [55] [Relative Time Intervals Representation for Word-level Timestamping with Masked Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24041)
*Quanwei Tang,Zhiyu Tang,Xu Li,Dong Zhang,Shoushan,Guodong Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用相对时间戳和带掩码的时间戳训练目标，通过嵌入/头部全量微调加上解码器LoRA的混合微调，把SpeechLLMs从纯内容理解扩展为具备时间感知的模型，提升时间对齐准确性同时维持转录质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SpeechLLMs擅长语言内容理解与生成，但缺乏精细且与时间轴强关联的输出（如逐词或逐标记时间戳）。通过引入时间感知能力，可扩展模型在字幕生成、强监督对齐、分段与检索等下游任务的适用性。

Method: 1) 将绝对时间戳替换为相对时间戳并将其离散化入模型词汇表；2) 在预训练模型上采用混合微调：对时间戳扩展的embedding和语言模型head做全量微调，对decoder层使用LoRA低秩适配；3) 设计掩码时间戳训练目标，随机屏蔽部分时间戳目标以避免模型过度依赖地面真值；4) 在若干数据集上评估时间戳预测准确率与转录错误率。

Result: 该工作提出通过相对时间戳和混合微调策略，使预训练的SpeechLLMs获得精细的、与时间对齐的输出能力，同时保持良好转录性能。相对时间戳减小词汇表和提升泛化，混合微调（嵌入层与头部全量更新 + 解码器LoRA）在参数效率和能力注入间取得平衡，掩码时间戳训练提高对噪声标注的鲁棒性。实验显示时间戳预测准确性显著提升且转录性能不退化。

Conclusion: 相对时间戳编码、受限位置更新与LoRA结合以及掩码时间戳训练是一种有效、参数高效且对噪声鲁棒的方案，可显著增强SpeechLLM的时间对齐能力，同时不会显著损害语音识别质量。

Abstract: Although Speech Large Language Models (SpeechLLMs) excel at speech understanding and generation, their capacity for fine-grained, temporally aligned outputs remains underexplored. Our work addresses this gap by enabling SpeechLLMs to jointly model speech content and temporal structure, effectively transforming them from ``content understanding machines" into ``temporal-aware content understanding machines". Specifically, we replace traditional absolute timestamps with relative timestamps, achieving a more compact vocabulary and stronger generalization capabilities. To efficiently infuse timestamp prediction ability into pre-trained large language models, we introduce a hybrid fine-tuning strategy: full-parameter fine-tuning of the timestamp-augmented embedding layer and language model head, combined with LoRA fine-tuning of the decoder layers. Moreover, we design a masked timestamp training objective, preventing the model from over-relying on ground-truth timestamps, and thereby enhancing robustness against noisy real-world annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves significant improvements in timestamp prediction accuracy while maintaining strong speech transcription performance.

</details>


### [56] [StarHarness: Evolving Harnesses with Stratified Search for Enterprise Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24804)
*Esakkivel Esakkiraja,Denis Akhiyarov,Vikas Yadav,Sai Rajeswar,Patrice Bechard,Sridhar Nemala,Sagar Davasam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StarHarness evolves environment-specific agent harnesses (prompts, interfaces, skills, etc.) using a stratified, pooled search with held-out evaluation, producing large robustness gains (20–35 pp) and transfer across models without weight updates.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Persistent mismatches between large language models and specific environments (tool-rich enterprise tasks) reduce performance even when model weights are fixed. Evolving harnesses that adapt to environment conventions and interfaces can close this gap without retraining models.

Method: StarHarness evolves agent harnesses by maintaining a compact evolution pool stratified by baseline failures, separating proposer-visible search tasks from proposer-hidden selection tasks, reserving held-out tasks for generalization evaluation, and iteratively accepting changes (4-12 per environment). The harness can modify prompts, tool interfaces, skills, subagents, providers, and agent-loop settings.

Result: Across ITBench SRE, EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM, and AutomationBench Finance, evolved harnesses yielded 20-35 percentage point improvement over default harness performance after a small number of changes. Gains persisted on held-out tasks and transferred across GPT and Qwen models. Trace analysis showed reduced false positives and shorter trajectories.

Conclusion: StarHarness is effective at evolving harnesses that significantly improve agent performance in environment-specific, tool-rich tasks without changing model weights.

Abstract: We present StarHarness, a framework for evolving environment-specific agent harnesses while keeping model weights fixed. The evolved harness can include prompt and task framing, tool interfaces, skills, MCP-backed providers, subagent structure, and agent-loop configuration. StarHarness constructs a compact evolution pool by stratifying tasks according to baseline failure behavior, separates proposer-visible search tasks from proposer-hidden selection tasks, and reserves held-out tasks for evaluating generalization. Across ITBench SRE, EnterpriseOps-Gym ITSM, and AutomationBench Finance, harness evolution improves full-benchmark performance by 20-35 percentage points over the default harness after 4-12 accepted changes per environment. These gains persist on tasks excluded from evolution and transfer without re-evolution across GPT and Qwen model families. Trace analysis links the improvements to interface repairs, environment conventions, and operational knowledge that compresses search, with fewer false-positive diagnoses and shorter trajectories in several settings. StarHarness therefore offers a practical way to reduce persistent model-environment mismatch in tool-rich enterprise tasks.

</details>


### [57] [Algorithmic Impact Reveals the Hidden Social Choice Structure of Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24046)
*Zachary Wojtowicz,Michelle Si,Finale Doshi-Velez,Ariel Procaccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把多主体AI对齐问题变换为凸影响空间上的线性优化，从而用福利经济学与机制设计工具构造并验证具策略性与伤害约束的对齐协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿AI对齐方法（如通过人类反馈的强化学习）在多主体偏好冲突时并未正面解决社会选择问题，缺乏明确的社会选择保证；为确保AI在影响多人时兼顾社会福利与个体/群体约束，需要一个既可解释又具形式性质证明的框架。

Method: 将算法决策对不同个体的影响编码为向量并形成一个凸的“影响空间”，把对齐目标表达为该空间上的线性优化问题；利用福利经济学与机制设计中的工具（如策略不可操纵性、无偏见性、功利最大化、约束最优化）来分析对齐协议的性质并从影响表示反向推导可执行的学习/对齐流程；对若干机制证明策略性与一致性性质，并在现实偏好数据集上进行数值实验以展示福利权衡。

Result: 提出并形式化了影响空间与线性优化框架；证明若干机制（按议题投票、随机独裁等）的策略不可操纵与一致性；从影响表示导出一族满足各种社会愿望（例如对个体或群体伤害的上界）并最大化功利社会福利的对齐协议；用真实人类偏好数据进行了实验验证，展示不同对齐选择的福利与伤害权衡。

Conclusion: 将AI对多人的决策对齐问题重构为在凸影响空间上的线性优化，使之可用福利经济学与机制设计的标准工具来分析与构造对齐协议；据此给出若干满足策略不可操纵（strategyproof）与一致性（unanimity）性质的机制（如按议题投票和随机独裁），并构造出在满足个体或群体损害约束下最大化功利主义社会福利的一族对齐协议；通过肾源分配、慈善食物分配、LLM回答与道德困境等真实人类偏好数据展示了这些协议的福利影响。

Abstract: When an AI algorithm makes decisions that affect more than one person, aligning it becomes a problem of social choice: how should people's divergent preferences about system behavior be reconciled and aggregated into a single coherent model? The standard approach to aligning frontier AI models$\unicode{x2013}$reinforcement learning from human feedback$\unicode{x2013}$largely sidesteps this question and has poor social choice guarantees. However, it remains unclear what alternative should replace it. We show that, by focusing directly on an algorithm's welfare consequences, the alignment problem can be reformulated as linear optimization over a convex impact space, which makes it amenable to the standard toolkit of welfare economics and mechanism design. This reformulation clarifies how alignment protocols translate into welfare consequences and, conversely, how a social planner's desired constraints on welfare consequences can be translated back into alignment protocols. We apply this transformation to show that voting-by-issues and random-dictatorship mechanisms are strategyproof and unanimous. Demonstrating the reverse direction, we also apply the impact representation to derive a family of alignment protocols that maximize utilitarian social welfare subject to various social desiderata, such as bounds on individual or group harm. We illustrate the welfare implications of these alignment protocols empirically using real human preferences over kidney allocation, charitable food distribution, LLM responses, and trolley problems.

</details>


### [58] [Poisoning Agentic Alpha: Adversarial Vulnerabilities Across Roles and Architectures in Multi-Agent Trading Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24069)
*CheolWon Na,Hao Ni,Lukasz Szpruch,Zhangyang Wang,Dhagash Mehta,Saurabh Nagrecha,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Chanyeol Choi,Yongjae Lee,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对角色化、低权限的输入干扰，作者系统评估了多智能体交易系统中攻击信号如何进入并传播到交易决策，发现无单一架构能保证稳健性，并提出APS用于量化信号保存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大模型的多智能体交易系统从研究原型走向控制真实资产的实盘部署，系统间的结构化通信既带来协同能力，也放大了通过篡改输入信号影响最终交易决策的风险。作者关注实际可达的低门槛威胁模型（只能修改代理消费的源数据和提示），并以角色化对手的形式研究攻击传播路径与影响范围。

Method: 按功能角色划分系统（Analyst/Researcher/Trader/Risk Manager），针对每个角色的接口设计特定攻击（源数据与提示层面）；对比四种通信拓扑；使用APS衡量攻击信号在各角色间的保留程度；在多标的、双主干模型和双攻击方向的设置下进行定量实验，评估最终决策受损程度与信号保持。

Result: 本文提出并实施了在金融交易域的首个系统性实证研究：将常用交易流水线拆分为分析员、研究员、交易员、风控四类角色，为每类接口设计匹配攻击；考察四种通信拓扑在数据级和代理级攻击下的表现；引入Adversarial Signal Preservation Score（APS）作为衡量攻击信号保存与传播的后验指标；在五种标的、两种模型主干和两个攻击方向上进行实验。核心结论是不存在天生稳健的架构，不同拓扑与角色有不同脆弱面。

Conclusion: 研究为未来设计更安全的代理化交易系统提供了经验性洞见：需要在架构层面和接口层面引入防护（输入净化、验证代理、多样性与仲裁机制等），并将攻击传播路径纳入风险评估与测试流程。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent trading systems, in which specialized agents collaborate through structured communication to produce trading decisions, are moving rapidly from research prototypes to live deployments that control real assets. The same inter-agent communication that makes them effective also exposes them: a corrupted signal can propagate to the final decision and translate into realized financial loss. Unlike prior attacks that presume privileged access to system internals, we restrict the adversary to what is practically reachable---the source data and prompts agents consume---yielding a low-barrier, and thus democratized threat model instantiated as role-specific adversaries.
  We present the first systematic empirical study in the financial domain to characterize how an adversarial signal enters a multi-agent trading system and how far it survives toward the decision. Along the role axis, we decompose a widely-used trading pipeline into four functional roles---Analyst, Researcher, Trader, and Risk Manager---and pair each with an attack matched to its interface. Along the structural axis, we evaluate four communication topologies under data- and agent-level attacks, using the Adversarial Signal Preservation Score (APS) as a post-hoc lens on why some designs are more robust than others. We conduct experiments across five assets, two backbones, and two target directions. A central finding is that no architecture is inherently robust. These findings provide insights for the future design of safer and more robust agentic trading systems.

</details>


### [59] [Compression Trinity: Exploring Sparsity, Quantization, and Low-Rank Approximations for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24070)
*Mohammad Mozaffari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过同时使用稀疏化、量化与低秩恢复，本文提出的多种方法在训练加速与压缩后精度恢复上都取得显著改进，证明三者协同能突破单一方法的效率-精度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型面临不可持续的计算与环境成本，现有单一压缩手段在效率与精度之间存在上限，需探索三者协同以突破瓶颈。

Method: 提出一套复合压缩框架并在不同阶段设计具体方法：MKOR 在优化器中用块对角稀疏与低秩逆近似加速曲率更新；SLoPe 在反向传播中使用双剪枝（N:M 稀疏）并在训练尾声用低秩“懒”适配器恢复精度；OPTIMA 在零训练情景下通过逐列二次规划构建稳定静态掩码；PATCH 在微调预算内学习动态混合稀疏比；SLiM 一次性将三者结合并用数学推导的低秩适配器恢复因量化与稀疏丢失的信息。

Result: 各组件在论文中报道的亮点包括：MKOR 比 KFAC 收敛快至 1.85x；SLoPe 将训练加速至 1.25x；OPTIMA 在零训练下提升零样本精度最多 3.97%；PATCH 在有限微调预算下实现最高 1.38x 的加速；SLiM 在一次性压缩场景下比最先进压缩方法提升最多 5.66%，并在相同参数预算下超越稠密模型 0.6%。

Conclusion: 联合应用稀疏化、量化与低秩近似（“Compression Trinity”）能够在保持或提升准确性的同时，显著降低大模型的计算与存储代价，且在预训练、训练加速与后训练压缩的多个场景下均优于单一方法或现有对比方法。

Abstract: Prohibitive computational and environmental costs impede the scalable deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs). Traditional compression techniques (sparsity, quantization, low-rank approximations) are typically applied in isolation, and each hits an accuracy-efficiency wall. This thesis proposes the "Compression Trinity," a unified framework that applies the three pillars jointly: sparsity to reduce computation, quantization to minimize memory bandwidth, and low-rank approximations to recover accuracy. To accelerate pretraining, we apply the Trinity to the optimizer and model architecture. MKOR approximates curvature via block-diagonal sparsity and low-rank inversion, maintaining numerical stability for quantized states; it reduces curvature update complexity from $O(d^3)$ to $O(d^2)$ and accelerates convergence by up to 1.85x over KFAC. SLoPe accelerates training by up to 1.25x via a double-pruned backward pass for N:M sparsity, using low-rank "lazy" adapters in the final 1% of training to recover accuracy. For post-training compression, OPTIMA stabilizes static masks in a zero-training regime by formulating weight reconstruction as globally optimal column-wise quadratic programs, improving zero-shot accuracy by up to 3.97%. Given a fine-tuning budget, PATCH breaks the ceiling of static masks by learning a dynamic hybrid sparsity ratio between 0% and 50%, yielding up to 1.38x speedups. Finally, SLiM realizes the full Compression Trinity in one shot, using mathematically derived low-rank adapters to recover information lost to quantization and sparsity, improving accuracy by up to 5.66% over state-of-the-art methods and outperforming uncompressed dense models at equal parameter budgets by 0.6%. Together, these results show that jointly applying the Compression Trinity is essential for efficient, scalable, high-performance LLMs.

</details>


### [60] [AgentWorld: Personality-Aware Reliability Evaluation for Agentic Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24076)
*Gunja Agarwal,Arup Kumar Das,Arun Menon,Jitesh Chandra Mishra,Vignesh Divakaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentWorld通过人格化用户群与轨迹级对抗分析，补齐了代理评估的两项短板——人格多样性与轨迹脆弱性量化，并在实验中揭示显著的失败模式与攻击来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理评估多依赖脚本化、统一用户模拟，无法反映自然人格差异带来的行为多样性与轨迹级对抗脆弱性；需要一个可导出训练样本、可分类故障并能量化轨迹风险的综合评估框架。

Method: 构建了一个集成框架：(1)基于OCEAN五因素生成有状态工具使用的用户族群；(2)采用pass^k一致性度量，配以结构化故障分类、部分记分与双控接手验证；(3)支持六种微调导出格式的分数阈值数据导出；(4)实现Risk Analyser，做中间态脊椎快照、在四类任务感知扰动下进行蒙特卡洛回滚，并用ΔP/ΔT评分、Dempster–Shafer证据融合及Shapley攻击类归因量化风险。实验包括：10个OCEAN人格的对话分析代理（240评估判断）、5任务×4人格变体的客服代理、和对5任务的对抗应力测试。

Result: 实证结果显示人格变异揭示了跨域泄露、上下文漂移、质量差距（0.27分）及同一任务在不同人格间的通过率差异（50% vs 100%）；对抗应力测试表明存在预先的轨迹脆弱性（V_min=0.375未扰动时）且攻击主要来自系统/基础设施层（Shapley：系统46%、动作38%）；Risk Analyser提供了pass^k无法捕捉的轨迹级脆弱性度量。

Conclusion: AgentWorld通过引入人格驱动的用户群体与轨迹级对抗分析，填补了现有代理信息检索评估中缺乏自然个性差异与对抗脆弱性检测的空白；该框架能揭示脚本化统一测试无法暴露的失效模式并量化轨迹脆弱性与攻击归因。

Abstract: Evaluation of agentic information retrieval remains limited to scripted interactions with uniform users, missing both natural personality diversity and adversarial brittleness. We present AgentWorld, a simulation framework combining (i)Big Five (OCEAN) personality-driven user populations with stateful tool-use environments; (ii)the pass$^k$ consistency metric with structured fault classification, partial-credit scoring, and dual-control handoff verification; (iii)score-thresholded training-data export in six fine-tuning formats; and (iv)an adversarial Risk Analyser that snapshots required-intermediate-state spines, branches Monte-Carlo rollouts under four task-aware perturbation types, and quantifies risk via $ΔP / ΔT$ scoring, Dempster--Shafer evidence fusion, and Shapley attack-category attribution. Three experiments demonstrate the framework: a conversational analytics agent across 10 OCEAN personas (240 evaluator judgments); a customer-support agent across 5 tasks $\times$ 4 persona variants; and adversarial stress-testing of 5 tasks revealing pre-existing trajectory brittleness ($V_{\min}=0.375$ without perturbation) and tool/infrastructure-layer attack dominance (Shapley: 46% system, 38% action). Personality variation surfaces failure modes uniform testing cannot expose---cross-domain leakage, contextual drift, a 0.27-point quality gap, and 50% vs. 100% pass-rate across personas on the same task---while the Risk Analyser quantifies trajectory-level brittleness that pass$^k$ alone cannot measure.

</details>


### [61] [Are Android GUI Agents Robust Against Runtime Anomalies? AnTrap: Evaluating Agents in Dynamic Adversarial Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24099)
*Guo Gan,Yilun Zhao,Cong Chen,Jinbiao Wei,Tingyu Song,Zheyuan Yang,Lin Fu,Hong Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnTrap 通过系统化注入动态扰动评估 Android GUI 代理鲁棒性，证明大多数模型在真实运行时异常下显著退化，且仅靠对抗训练无法完全应对深层语境陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是补足现有基准在评估运行期动态异常与代理鲁棒性方面的空白，为现实部署（如 Android 设备）中的不可预见弹窗、误操作等提供系统化测试与防护方向。

Method: 方法包括：构建四层（State, Thinking, Action, Round）十类细粒度异常分类法；设计扰动注入流水线以保证任务仍可解；在基准上评测 16 个模型并量化性能降幅；用 GRPO（对抗 RL）在干净与对抗环境中训练并对比收益以区分环境可学与推理受限的陷阱。
