This design enables a dual-granularity inference mechanism that dynamically fuses attribute evidence for both fine-grained and coarse-grained downstream tasks. Furthermore, we release a comprehensive fine-grained e-commerce dataset to facilitate future benchmarking. Extensive experiments on this new dataset and the public MAVE benchmark show that HMGCLIP outperforms strong multimodal encoders, MLLMs, and e-commerce baselines, validating the superiority of HMGCLIP.

</details>


### [93] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided Evolutionary Policy Optimization for Preference-Adjustable Heterogeneous Agile Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24470)
*He Wang,Junyu Wu,Hui Li,Yanjie Song,Witold Pedrycz,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将编码/解码的可解释建模与强化学习驱动算子选择相结合的进化策略，用于异构AEOS调度，实验证明比传统元启发式方法表现更好且更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构灵活地球观测卫星调度中的复杂性，包括卫星依赖的可见窗、姿态机动、能耗与存储等约束，以及不同卫星对同一任务在可行窗口、转移代价和资源消耗上的差异，导致统一建模与高效优化困难。

Method: 在建模层使用分配型间接编码+解码器等价成本评估，将任务收益、能量节省与负载均衡整合为可解释标量效用；在优化层将调度解码、基于种群的搜索与在线actor-critic算子控制解耦，强化学习用于选择高层搜索算子而非直接构造调度，形成RL辅助算子选择的记忆进化算法（RLOSMEA）。

Result: 提出基于演化策略优化的框架并实现RLOSMEA——一种结合分配型间接编码、解码等价成本评估与强化学习指导算子选择的记忆进化算法。在多场景实验中，RLOSMEA在加权效用、收敛稳定性和鲁棒性方面均优于代表性元启发式基线，敏感性与学习行为分析证实了其稳健性与RL算子选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦调度解码、种群搜索与在线actor-critic算子控制，框架能够在有限评价预算下协调全局探索、可行性修复与局部改进，有效处理卫星异构性并提高加权效用与收敛性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous agile Earth observation satellite (AEOS) scheduling requires task selection, satellite assignment, and observation sequencing under satellite-dependent visibility windows, attitude maneuvering requirements, energy consumption, and onboard storage constraints. Since satellites differ in orbital access, maneuvering capability, and payload resources, the same task may have different feasible windows, transition costs, and resource-consumption patterns on different platforms, which increases the difficulty of unified modeling and efficient optimization. To address this problem, this paper proposes an evolutionary policy optimization framework for heterogeneous AEOS scheduling with preference-adjustable weighted objectives. In the modeling layer, assignment-based indirect encoding is combined with decoder-based equivalent-cost evaluation to retain satellite-dependent constraints while integrating task gain, energy saving, and load balance into an interpretable scalar utility. In the optimization layer, schedule decoding, population-based search, and online actor-critic operator control are decoupled, so that reinforcement learning selects high-level search operators rather than constructing schedules directly. Based on this framework, a reinforcement-learning-assisted operator-selection memetic evolutionary algorithm (RLOSMEA) is developed to coordinate global exploration, feasibility recovery, and local refinement under a limited function-evaluation budget. Experiments on different heterogeneous AEOS scenarios show that RLOSMEA achieves higher overall weighted utility and more stable convergence than representative metaheuristic baselines. Sensitivity and learning-behavior analyses further confirm the robustness of the proposed method and the effectiveness of reinforcement-learning-guided operator selection.

</details>


### [94] [Implicit Q-learning-bootstrapped ant colony optimization for maritime moving-target observation scheduling with agile satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24471)
*He Wang,Junyu Wu,Yeye Liu,Yifan Zhou,Jie Zhang,Hui Li,Yanjie Song,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将离线隐式Q学习嵌入蚁群算法以自适应调参，有效提升多卫星海面移动目标观测调度的解质量与收敛速度，较传统蚁群提升3.4%–9.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海面目标连续移动且观测窗随目标与卫星几何关系变化，问题是序列相关、动态且受时间窗、姿态机动、机载资源、云量等多重约束，传统固定参数启发式搜索难以长期保持高效，需引入能基于搜索状态自适应调控的机制。

Method: 在离线阶段训练隐式Q学习模块，利用压缩的搜索状态（信息素分布、当前与历史最优解质量、迭代进度）学习价值函数；在线阶段，蚁群算法负责构造可行观测序列，学习到的隐式Q值用于自适应调节信息素强度、启发式因子与挥发率，以动态控制探索与利用的权衡。

Result: 在14个不同规模与卫星配置场景上评测，IQACO在每个场景均取得最高平均观测收益，比常规模型提升3.40%–9.40%，收敛速度加快且在不同目标权重设置下表现稳定，证明离线价值学习可作为约束调度问题的有效自适应搜索控制机制。

Conclusion: IQACO通过将离线隐式Q学习嵌入到构造型蚁群算法中，自适应地调整信息素因子、启发因子及挥发率，从而在多卫星海上移动目标观测调度问题上取得了优于传统蚁群算法的性能，平均观测收益在所有测试场景中最高，比传统蚁群算法提升3.40%–9.40%，收敛更快且在目标权重变化下稳定。

Abstract: Maritime moving-target observation scheduling with agile Earth observation satellites is a dynamic, sequence-dependent combinatorial optimization problem. Sea-surface targets move continuously, causing feasible observation windows to vary with target motion and satellite orbital geometry. The scheduler must jointly determine task selection, satellite assignment, observation-window selection, and observation ordering under time-window, attitude-maneuvering, onboard-resource, and cloud-affected availability constraints. This paper proposes an implicit Q-learning-bootstrapped ant colony optimization method, termed IQACO, for multi-satellite maritime moving-target observation scheduling. Rather than directly learning a task-selection policy, IQACO embeds an offline implicit Q-learning module into constructive ant colony optimization to adaptively adjust the pheromone factor, heuristic factor, and evaporation rate. A compact search-state representation captures pheromone distribution, current and historical-best solution quality, and iteration progress. During online scheduling, ant colony optimization constructs feasible observation sequences, while the learned policy regulates exploration and exploitation according to the current search state. Experiments on 14 scenarios with different scales and satellite configurations show that IQACO obtains the highest mean observation benefit in every scenario, improves the result of conventional ant colony optimization by 3.40\%--9.40\%, accelerates convergence, and remains stable under different objective-weight settings. These results demonstrate that offline value learning provides an effective adaptive search-control mechanism for constrained maritime moving-target observation scheduling.

</details>


### [95] [Neurosymbolic Alignment for Physiologically-Safe Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24534)
*Abdulhady Abas Abdullah,Erik Cambria,Milena Zivkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在训练时将LLM与大型生理知识图谱驱动的HGNN世界模型对齐，并用生理约束对候选输出重排序并迭代更新，论文在受控基准上显著提升了临床生成建议的生理安全性，需要在真实临床数据上做外部验证以评估部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床LLM在生成看似合理但生理上不安全的建议时风险高，文本监督不足以捕捉结构化生理知识与因果约束，故尝试将结构化生理知识用于偏好优化以提高安全性。

Method: 提出Neurosymbolic Alignment：将7B临床LLM与覆盖847K节点的生物医学知识图谱上的HGNN生理世界模型耦合，使用稳态约束、多跳路径可信度和药物相互作用惩罚对候选回复打分，基于这些排名进行迭代的on-policy ORPO更新。

Result: 在Clinical Safety Benchmark上相较ORPO将CSS从69.5%提升到90.8%，医师评估下心率错误从14.1%降至5.1%，DID从72.8%升至91.6%；与HGNN无关的规则引擎RSS一致性高（r=0.97）；超越GPT-4(5-shot)及SFT+SelfCorrect基线；在合成噪声下保持84.2% CSS；消融显示HGNN评分与迭代训练是主要贡献者；PhysioScore校准良好(ECE=0.038，kappa=0.91)。

Conclusion: 训练时将临床LLM与基于HGNN的生理世界模型耦合，通过生理约束及多跳路径可行性评分驱动偏好优化，可显著降低生理学不安全建议，并在受控基准上独立验证该改进的可靠性。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs can generate recommendations that are factually plausible yet physiologically unsafe. We investigate whether safety alignment can be improved by grounding preference optimization in structured physiological knowledge rather than text-only supervision. Methods: We propose Neurosymbolic Alignment, a training-time framework that couples a 7B clinical LLM with an HGNN-based Physiological World Model over an 847K-node biomedical knowledge graph. Candidate responses are scored using homeostatic constraints, multi-hop path plausibility, and drug-interaction penalties, and the resulting rankings drive iterative on-policy ORPO updates. Evaluation is performed on the Clinical Safety Benchmark (CSB), a 2,500-scenario benchmark for physiological constraint violations in generative clinical reasoning. Results: Relative to ORPO, the proposed method improves CSS from 69.5% to 90.8% (+21.3 pp), reduces physician-evaluated HR from 14.1% to 5.1% on the blinded subset, and improves DID from 72.8% to 91.6%. These gains are corroborated by an HGNN-independent Rule-Engine Safety Score (RSS: 86.4%, +21.2 pp over ORPO; r=0.97 concordance with CSS). The method also exceeds GPT-4 (5-shot) on all safety metrics despite a 10x parameter disadvantage, and outperforms an inference-time self-correction pipeline (SFT+SelfCorrect) by 11.4 pp CSS. Under synthetic EHR-style noise, 84.2% CSS is retained. Ablation analysis shows that HGNN scoring (-16.2 pp) and iterative training (-11.5 pp) are the dominant contributors. PhysioScore calibration against 200 clinician labels yielded ECE = 0.038 and kappa = 0.91. Conclusion: Training-time physiological grounding produces measurable and independently verifiable safety improvements in open-weight clinical LLMs under controlled evaluation. External validation on real clinical data is required to determine whether these gains transfer to deployment settings

</details>


### [96] [Discovering Adaptive Transmission Programs for Collective Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24545)
*Cédric Colas,Jérémy Perez,Eleni Nisioti,Akhilesh Mocherla,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer,Clément Moulin-Frier,Maxime Derex*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Designing transmission protocols that condition routing on agent/collective states and discovering them with LLM-guided evolutionary search yields sizable, generalizable improvements in collective discovery, highlighting a promising direction for AI-assisted coordination-design.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior literature varied network topology and timing but treated networks as state-agnostic; the work asks whether allowing transmission to depend on content/state can yield better collective outcomes and whether such protocols can be found algorithmically.

Method: Formalize transmission protocols as state-aware programs that route information/resources based on agent-level and collective states; use an evolutionary search loop guided by large language models to propose, mutate, and select protocol candidates in a simulated collective discovery task; evaluate protocols against literature baselines, perform ablations removing content-dependence, and test transfer across domain and agent-population variations.

Result: Evolved, state-aware protocols outperform standard baselines by up to 37%; ablations show removing content-dependence removes gains; evolved protocols transfer to other domains and agent populations, demonstrating robustness and generalizability.

Conclusion: State-aware transmission protocols, discovered via LLM-guided evolutionary search, substantially improve collective discovery performance over standard (state-agnostic) network-based baselines; the performance gains (up to 37%) depend on conditioning routing on agent and collective state, and the evolved protocols generalize across domain variations and agent populations, indicating the feasibility of AI-assisted design of coordination infrastructure.

Abstract: Human collective intelligence depends on transmission processes: who shares what with whom, how, and when. While these processes emerge from individual cognition, they can also be directed by deliberate top-down protocols. Prior work has studied how transmission shapes collective outcomes primarily through the lens of network structure, varying who shares with whom and when. But networks are state-agnostic: they cannot condition transmission on what agents know or on the state of the collective. Here, we formalize transmission protocols as state-aware programs that route information and resources based on agent and collective states, and we use LLM-guided evolutionary search to design effective protocols in a collective discovery task. Evolved protocols increase collective performance over standard baselines from the literature by up to 37%. Ablations confirm that state-awareness drives this advantage: removing content-dependence while preserving network topology and timing eliminates performance gains. We find that evolved protocols also transfer across domain variations and agent populations. These results demonstrate that effective and generalizable transmission protocols can be discovered in silico, suggesting a path toward AI-assisted design of coordination infrastructure that enhances human collective intelligence.

</details>


### [97] [When "Must" Becomes "Maybe": Constraint Weakening in LLM Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24569)
*Yiheng Sun,Huifei Wang,Yancheng Zhu,Zhenyu Li,Zebin Zhao,Yifan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Semantic content can survive a handoff while losing its action-binding force; explicit preservation of binding fields or downstream verification is needed to prevent unsafe actions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To study how multi-role, multi-stage language artifacts transmit not only topical information but operational constraints (action-binding state) that are necessary to safely constrain downstream agent actions, and to identify failure modes where semantic availability does not imply operational preservation.

Method: Controlled synthetic evaluation using 1,296 episodes where upstream safety 'blockers' (each with prerequisite, authority, fallback, execution consequence) are correctly identified, then transformed via several handoff strategies (direct handoff, compression, plan assimilation, convergence, ownership deferral, precedent substitution). An executor is restricted to act using only the resulting artifact; outcomes (preservation, deactivation, forbidden action) are measured.

Result: Direct handoff preserves all blocking state; common transformations like compression or assimilation frequently convert binding state into non-binding caveats, yielding 100% deactivation in normal compression and significant rates of forbidden actions (e.g., 54.2%). Restoring all four binding fields recovers preservation and eliminates forbidden actions. Downstream verification prevents forbidden actions even when artifacts are deactivated, showing separation between preservation and containment.

Conclusion: Handoff transformations commonly used in LLM-agent workflows often preserve semantic content but fail to preserve operational constraints; this leads to deactivation of action-binding state and enables forbidden downstream actions unless all binding fields are explicitly preserved or downstream verification is applied.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents coordinate complex tasks through multi-role and multi-stage workflows. Upstream state is repeatedly transformed into intermediate language artifacts, such as summaries, plans, tickets, memories, and handoff notes, from which downstream components act. For action-constraining state, topical retention is insufficient: an artifact may mention an unresolved condition while changing it from a requirement that must be resolved before execution into information that may merely inform the next action. We study this action-binding role as operational state preservation. Safety blockers provide a controlled instance because each source state has an explicit prerequisite, authority, fallback, and execution consequence. We condition on correct upstream identification, vary the handoff transformation, and evaluate an executor restricted to the resulting artifact. Across 1,296 controlled synthetic episodes, direct-handoff controls preserve every blocker, whereas compression, plan assimilation, convergence, ownership deferral, and precedent substitution repeatedly turn binding state into caveats or non-binding considerations. Normal handoff compression produces 100.0% deactivation and 54.2% forbidden action. Restoring all four state fields raises preservation to 100.0% and reduces forbidden action to 0.0%. Fixed-artifact interventions further separate preservation from containment: downstream verification eliminates forbidden action while artifact deactivation remains 95.3%. These results identify a state-transmission failure between information extraction and action. Handoff transformations can retain state content while weakening its constraints on downstream action. Semantic availability does not guarantee operational preservation.

</details>


### [98] [EviDx: Evidence-Aware Active Diagnosis with Scaffolded LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24570)
*Lihang Zeng,Shaoting Zhang,Xiaofan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EviDx 通过交互式环境、角色化脚手架和观察者驱动的运行控制，把诊断从静态预测变为证据驱动的主动过程，提升了性能并展示了模型的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于临床诊断是一个主动的证据获取与假设更新过程，但现有 LLM 驱动的诊断系统多将诊断视为静态的病例–答案预测，缺乏对证据暴露、支架化与运行时控制的体系化支持。

Method: 方法由三部分组成： (1) E- Synthesis：从原始临床病例构建可交互的诊断环境； (2) 诊断脚手架：组织角色专门化的代理、证据工具与不断演化的证据状态；(3) 运行哈尼斯：由观察者跟踪不确定性与证据覆盖来调控诊断终止。并通过三层评估金字塔（执行健壮性、推理动力学、诊断结果）评估系统。

Result: 实验证明 EviDx 在诊断性能和过程稳定性上有提升，同时暴露出不同模型在主动证据获取与推理轨迹管理上的能力边界。

Conclusion: EviDx 提出了一种以证据为中心的主动诊断框架，通过构建交互式病人诊断环境、角色化的诊断脚手架与观察者驱动的运行控制，改善了基于大模型的诊断过程的性能与稳定性，并揭示了模型能力的边界。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is an active evidence-seeking process in which clinicians acquire evidence, update competing hypotheses, and decide when the available evidence is sufficient for diagnosis. Yet many medical diagnosis systems built around large language models (LLMs) still formulate diagnosis as static case-to-answer prediction, with limited support for evidence acquisition. Agentic LLMs offer a dynamic alternative through tool use and intermediate diagnostic trajectories, but existing systems often under-specify how patient evidence should be exposed, scaffolded, and controlled at runtime. We introduce EviDx, an evidence-aware active diagnosis framework that pairs patient-specific diagnostic environments with a clinical diagnostic scaffold and an observer-guided runtime harness. In EviDx, $\mathcal{E}$-Synthesis constructs interactive environments from raw clinical cases; the scaffold organizes role-specialized agents, evidence tools, and evolving evidence states; and the harness regulates diagnostic termination by tracking uncertainty and evidence coverage. A 3-level evaluation pyramid assesses execution robustness, reasoning dynamics, and diagnostic outcomes. Experiments show that EviDx improves diagnostic performance and process stability while revealing model-dependent capability boundaries.

</details>


### [99] [PhysMLLMs: Spatial Priors for Unified Referring Segmentation and Grounded Reasoning of Images and Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24574)
*Siyao Yan,Bo Han,Jisheng Dang,Bimei Wang,Shude Wang,Hong Peng,Yulan Guo,Jianhuang Lai,Bin Hu,Tat-SengChua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在训练中对齐学生的全局视觉表示到冻结的DINOv2教师（离线缓存+调度蒸馏），注入空间连续性先验，PhysMLLMs在不增加推理开销的前提下改善视频分割的时空稳定性，同时保持图像级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频多模态大模型在语言引导的视频分割任务中存在时空不一致性（抖动、漂移、身份切换），原因可能是训练中缺乏显式的空间连续性先验，难以维持稳定的目标中心化表示与形状随时间的一致性。

Method: 训练阶段的先验注入架构：1) 冻结DINOv2作为教师，提取并离线缓存全局视觉嵌入；2) 设计调度蒸馏计划，在训练中用REPA-Global将教师嵌入对齐到学生的全局视觉表示；3) 不改变模型结构与推理流程，所有改动仅限于训练期间的目标与损失。

Result: 提出PhysMLLMs：在训练阶段注入物理启发的空间连续性先验，通过REPA-Global将冻结的DINOv2教师的全局视觉表示蒸馏到学生模型，使用离线嵌入缓存与调度蒸馏计划；保持推理不变且无额外推理成本，在多视频基准上显著提升分割掩码质量和跨帧一致性，尤其在小目标、快速运动、遮挡、干扰对象和需要推理的场景中。单帧指代分割与通用VLM基准性能保持可比，证明不损害图像级能力。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 物理启发的空间先验注入是一种有效的训练阶段策略，可在不牺牲通用性与推理效率的情况下提高视频MLLM的时间一致性与分割质量。

Abstract: Video multimodal large language models support language guided video segmentation, but they often show spatio temporal inconsistencies, e.g., jitter, drift, and identity switches. These failures are more common when targets are partly hidden or when similar objects appear nearby.One likely reason is that current training lacks explicit spatial priors, which makes it difficult to maintain stable spatial identity and shape over time. We present PhysMLLMs, a training-stage prior injection architecture that injects physics-inspired spatial continuity priors into Video MLLMs. PhysMLLMs is designed to encourage more stable object-centered representations by aligning the student global visual representation with a frozen teacher model during training. Our core mechanism, Global Representation Prior Alignment (REPA-Global), distills global visual representations from a frozen DINOv2 teacher using an offline embedding cache and a scheduled distillation plan. This design keeps inference unchanged and does not add inference time cost. Across multiple video benchmarks, PhysMLLMs improves video segmentation mask quality and cross-frame consistency, with larger gains on challenging cases involving small targets, fast motion, occlusion, distractors, and reasoning queries. On single-frame referring image segmentation and representative general VLM benchmarks, PhysMLLMs maintains comparable performance, demonstrating that the injected spatial prior improves video consistency without compromising image-level grounding or general multimodal capability. These results suggest that physics-inspired spatial prior injection can improve temporal stability while preserving general capability. The code is available at https://github.com/tusu-code/20260121-icml2026-2.git.

</details>


### [100] [Pivot-and-Station Multi-Agent Path Finding: Solvability, Complexity, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24585)
*Andrea Di Nezza,Mihir Patel,Fabio Fagnani,Sara Bernardini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduces PS-MAPF (agents must visit a pivot then park at anonymous stations), fully characterizes solvability, proves NP-hardness for key optimization goals, and offers practical algorithms—PPP achieves high empirical success and strong objective improvements.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Practical automated high-density storage and logistics systems require agents to visit interchangeable work pivots and then park at stations without blocking future operations, a requirement not captured by standard MAPF formulations.

Method: Formulate Pivot-and-Station MAPF formally; analyze graph-structural properties to derive solvability theorems; reduce known NP-hard problems to prove hardness of optimization objectives; design three algorithms (complete baseline, SAT-based optimal solver, and Pivot-Prioritized Planning heuristic); empirically evaluate on benchmark instances measuring solvability rate, makespan, and flowtime.

Result: Theoretical: full solvability characterization on classes of graphs and an effective-distance necessary-and-sufficient condition; Complexity: NP-hardness for minimizing station-makespan/flowtime even with one pivot; Empirical: PPP solves 74–89% of benchmarks and achieves makespan/flowtime orders of magnitude better than baseline; SAT solver gives optimal solutions when feasible.

Conclusion: The paper defines PS-MAPF and gives a complete characterization of solvability (every instance on a 2-edge-connected graph is solvable; on arbitrary connected graphs solvability is characterized by an effective-distance measure relative to number of unoccupied vertices), proves NP-hardness of minimizing station-makespan or station-flowtime even with a single pivot, and provides three solvers including a practical heuristic (Pivot-Prioritized Planning) that solves most benchmarks with large improvements over a baseline.

Abstract: Automated high-density storage systems (warehouses, robotic parking, plant logistics, etc.) require fleets of agents to move through scarce task-critical resources and then park without obstructing future operations. We introduce Pivot-and-Station Multi-Agent Path Finding (PS-MAPF), a MAPF variant in which a subset of tasked agents must each visit one of a set of interchangeable pivots (e.g., workstations) before the entire fleet terminates at anonymous stations, one agent per station. We characterize solvability completely: every instance on a 2-edge-connected graph is solvable, and, on arbitrary connected graphs, a structural effective-distance measure relative to the number of unoccupied vertices gives a necessary and sufficient condition. We prove that minimizing station-makespan or station-flowtime is NP-hard already with a single pivot. We present three algorithms, a complete baseline, a SAT-based optimal solver, and Pivot-Prioritized Planning (PPP), the last solving 74-89% of benchmark instances with makespan and flowtime orders of magnitude below the baseline.

</details>


### [101] [Causal Modelling of Support Interventions for Student Competency Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24632)
*Francesca Mangili,Alessandro Antonucci,Rafael Cabañas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paper advocates SCMs for educational assessment, gives a practical expert-elicitation protocol to build causal models that support interventions and counterfactuals, and demonstrates feasibility using algorithmic-skills assessment data.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional psychometric models emphasize associative/probabilistic relations and belief updating but lack native support for modeling interventions and counterfactuals necessary for instructional decision-making and evaluating ‘what if’ scenarios.

Method: Propose a protocol to construct SCMs for assessment: define variables and DAG, elicit structural equations from experts (logical rules), model interventions (e.g., hints) and counterfactual queries, and apply methodology to data from complex algorithmic tasks.

Result: Protocol is feasible and yields reasoning forms unavailable to associative models—explicit intervention modelling and counterfactual scenario analysis—demonstrated on compulsory-school algorithmic-skills assessment data; expert elicitation was practical and required logical rather than probabilistic judgements.

Conclusion: Adopting structural causal models (SCMs) for educational assessment enables explicit interventional and counterfactual reasoning that standard psychometric models (e.g., IRT) cannot provide; expert-elicited structural equations suffice without relying on probabilistic assumptions; applied example demonstrates practicality for assessing algorithmic skills.

Abstract: Accurate assessment of student competencies is essential for enabling educators to identify individual needs, design targeted interventions, and evaluate the effectiveness of educational strategies. Empirical assessment procedures are typically grounded in psychometric models, such as item response theory, which relate student competence levels to performance on assessment tasks. In this paper, we advocate adopting a structural causal modelling approach to educational assessment, moving beyond probabilistic belief updating toward a framework that explicitly supports interventional and counterfactual reasoning. We propose a corresponding protocol for its construction and analyse the practical relevance of forms of reasoning that remain inaccessible to standard associative models, including the explicit modelling of interventions such as hints and the related counterfactual scenario analysis. Although our protocol requires the structural equations to be elicited from experts, the necessary information is purely logical and does not rely on probabilistic, less tenable assumptions. We illustrate the approach using data from an assessment that employs complex tasks designed to measure compulsory school student algorithmic skills.

</details>


### [102] [Parason: Revealing Subtask and Trial Parallelism in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24658)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Zijian Zhang,Jiaxuan Gao,Shusheng Xu,Yi Wu,Song Han,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Parason 通过识别并学习子任务并行与试验并行，结合基于文法的并行轨迹表示与PA-GRPO训练，实现了在真实推理环境下的可观墙钟加速（约1.7×）且不显著损失准确率，指出试验并行是主要可并行资源并且对困难问题尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自回归解码在长推理链上造成极高延迟；先前工作侧重子任务并行而忽视了普遍存在的试验并行（并行探索多种假设并汇总），需要系统性地发现并学习两种并行性以缩短真实世界的推理时间。

Method: 将顺序推理轨迹转为基于上下文无关文法的结构化并行轨迹；提出并行感知的组相对策略优化（PA-GRPO），在训练中用一个兼顾准确率、延迟与两类并行比率的奖励函数优化模型；推理时通过工具调用执行学到的并行结构以获得实际的墙钟加速。

Result: 分析显示试验并行占可并行化推理计算的大头（在DeepSeek-V4于HLE上的推理步骤中为65.5%），在难题上更为显著；在数学推理基准（如AIME24/AIME25）上，Parason 在保持竞争性准确率的同时实现约1.7×的平均加速。

Conclusion: Parason 有效揭示并利用两类并行性（子任务并行与试验并行），尤其强调试验并行在难题上的主导地位，从而在保持准确率的同时实现显著的实际加速。

Abstract: Scaling test-time reasoning has substantially improved the problem-solving ability of large language models (LLMs), but standard autoregressive decoding still executes long reasoning traces sequentially, creating severe latency for difficult tasks (up to days and weeks). Parallel reasoning offers a natural remedy. However, prior systems primarily focus on Subtask Parallelism, where the model learns to decompose a high-level task into smaller chunks that can be solved independently. This approach overlooks another pervasive form of parallelism: Trial Parallelism, where multiple speculative attempts explore, verify, and aggregate competing hypotheses in parallel. In this paper, we introduce Parason, which reveals and learns both forms of parallelism in LLM reasoning. Our analysis identifies Trial Parallelism as the majority of parallelizable reasoning computation (65.5% in DeepSeek-V4's reasoning steps in HLE), and it becomes increasingly dominant on hard problems. Guided by this taxonomy, Parason converts sequential reasoning traces into structured parallel trajectories with a context-free grammar, then trains models with Parallelism-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (PA-GRPO), whose reward jointly balances accuracy, latency, and the two parallelism ratios. At inference time, Parason executes the learned parallel structure through tool calls, translating theoretical savings to real-world wall-clock acceleration. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks including AIME24 and AIME25 show that Parason achieves an average acceleration about 1.7$\times$ while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [The Invisible Editorial Layer: Formalizing Undisclosed Inference-Time Steering, Probability Placement, and the Attribution Problem in Deployed Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24662)
*Augusto Camargo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM behavior is not fully determined by weights; deployment-time logit/decoding interventions can stealthily bias outputs. The paper formalizes this risk, proposes governance concepts, and situates them in EU/FTC policy contexts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To expose and analyze a largely overlooked control layer—decoding/logit-level interventions applied after model inference—that can bias generated text politically, ideologically, institutionally, or commercially, with implications for governance, security, and economics.

Method: Conceptual analysis formalizing the distinction between model and deployed system; definition of Inference Attribution Problem, Probability Placement, and Inference Policy Transparency; legal-policy mapping to EU AI Act, DSA, and FTC doctrines; conceptual examples referencing decoding-level interventions and watermarking systems.

Result: Introduces formal concepts, frames risks (misattribution, covert advertising, regulatory gaps), and recommends Inference Policy Transparency as a governance principle tied to existing laws; demonstrates technical plausibility by linking to existing methods (PPLM, GeDi, DExperts, FUDGE, SynthID-Text).

Conclusion: The paper argues that observed LLM outputs can be systematically shaped by inference-time interventions, so attributing behavior solely to model weights is incomplete; it calls for transparency and governance of deployment-layer policies.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are commonly evaluated under the assumption that their observable behavior is primarily determined by model weights, training data, alignment procedures, and user prompts. This view is incomplete. Modern inference pipelines may systematically modify the probability distribution produced by a model immediately before token selection, creating an additional layer of control between frozen weights and observed text.
  While controlled generation (e.g., PPLM, GeDi, DExperts, FUDGE) and text-watermarking systems (e.g., SynthID-Text) demonstrate the technical maturity of decoding- and logit-level interventions, the governance, security, and economic implications of an undisclosed inference policy remain comparatively underexplored. This paper examines the emergence of inference-time framing bias: the systematic modification of generated language toward political, ideological, institutional, or commercial frames via interventions applied after model inference but before token sampling.
  We formalize the operational reality Model != Deployed System and introduce three concepts: (1) the Inference Attribution Problem, characterizing why observed behavioral bias cannot generally be causally attributed to model weights alone under limited observability; (2) Probability Placement, defining a hypothetical advertising primitive in which commercial influence is implemented through systematic shifts in generation probabilities rather than explicit product insertions; and (3) Inference Policy Transparency, a governance principle for making deployment-layer interventions auditable. We examine these concepts in relation to Article 5 of the EU AI Act, the EU Digital Services Act, and FTC doctrines.

</details>


### [104] [Confident at the moment of action: belief miscalibration in LLM play under hidden information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24691)
*Bhushan Kashinath Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Models’ stated confidence can be massively miscalibrated at decision time—especially when they act—so using that confidence to gate actions is unsafe; calibration is sensitive to compute/deliberation and not captured by standard performance metrics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Evaluate whether agentic systems’ self-reported confidence reliably tracks correctness at decision time, since many systems gate actions on that confidence.

Method: Design a hidden-information chess variant with a secretly relocatable royal piece. On each turn, elicit the model’s probability distribution over the opponent’s hidden royal piece (separate from the chosen move). Score the elicited beliefs against ground-truth revealed after the game. Run two independent batches and additional model/configuration variations across providers; compare metrics across deliberation budgets and conventional evaluation axes.

Result: In a hidden-information chess task where models reported their probability distribution over the opponent’s hidden royal piece each turn, captures made when the model stated high confidence (>=0.5) were correct in only 1 of 62 cases. The calibration deficit is overwhelmingly concentrated in these high-confidence capture events (99.3% of the deficit in the original batch, 98.7% in the replication). The pattern persists, in weaker form, across multiple model configurations and a second provider. Deliberation-budget changes can move the metric nearly as much as large cross-model differences. Conventional performance metrics (legality, cost, latency, completion rate) can be entirely dissociated from belief quality; the best conventional-configured model had the worst belief quality. A model can still win the game despite badly calibrated beliefs.

Conclusion: Self-reported confidence from contemporary models is not a reliable indicator of correctness at the moment of action in this hidden-information domain; belief-quality needs explicit evaluation and calibration before being used for gating. Outcome-only evaluation and conventional system metrics can fail to reveal severe miscalibration.

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly gate actions on a model's own stated confidence, which assumes confidence tracks correctness at the moment of acting. We test this in a hidden-information chess variant where royal status can be secretly, repeatedly relocated between pieces, and where an agent's stated probability distribution over the opponent's hidden royal piece -- elicited every turn, separately from the move it chooses -- is scored against ground truth recoverable after the game. Across two independent batches, captures made at high stated confidence ($\geq 0.5$) about the hidden piece's location were correct in 1 of 62 cases. The calibration deficit is concentrated almost entirely in these events: 99.3% of it in the original batch, 98.7% in the replication. The same pattern, in weaker form, orders consistently (point estimates only; most pairwise gaps are not statistically distinguishable at this sample size) across four further model configurations spanning a second provider -- reported as scope for the finding, not as evidence that capability predicts calibration: a same-model comparison at a fixed external leaderboard score shows a deliberation-budget change alone moves the metric by nearly as much as a large cross-model gap. In a separate seat, conventional evaluation axes -- legality, cost, latency, completion rate -- can dissociate entirely from belief quality, with the configuration winning on every conventional axis producing the worst belief quality tested. A model exhibiting this pattern can still win the game its belief was about, which is why outcome-only evaluation would not detect it.

</details>


### [105] [Lifted Model Construction under Approximate Commutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24713)
*Malte Luttermann,Jan Speller,Tanya Braun,Marcel Gehrke,Ralf Möller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ε-可交换性概念并基于它构造提升模型，给出误差上界证明与实证结果，表明在参数存在小偏差时仍能实现高效且精确的提升推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提升推理依赖完全的因子可交换性，但从数据学习得到的参数通常会偏离完美对称性，导致无法或低效地利用提升表示。需要一种容忍小偏差同时维持可解释误差控制的方案，以便在真实数据情形下应用提升推理。

Method: 形式化定义ε-可交换性，提出基于该定义的提升模型构造与下游概率推理流程，并从理论上给出由ε-近似带来的误差上界。实现上可能通过将近似可交换的因子合并/压缩并调整推理算法以利用对称性近似来加速计算。

Result: 论文给出严格的理论保证（误差界），并通过实证实验验证：在保持查询精度可比的前提下，采用ε-可交换性构造的提升模型显著降低了运行时间。

Conclusion: 通过引入ε-可交换性的放宽定义，论文证明了即使因从数据学习导致参数有小偏差，仍能构造近似的提升（lifted）表示并保持查询结果的高精度。该方法在保持理论误差界的同时显著降低了推理运行时。

Abstract: Lifted inference algorithms enable scalable probabilistic inference even for large object domains by leveraging the indistinguishability of objects in a probability distribution. An essential prerequisite for constructing a lifted representation is to identify commutative factors, i.e., functions whose output values are invariant under permutations of a subset of their input values, in a potential-based factorisation. In practice, however, parameters learned from data inevitably deviate even if associated objects are indistinguishable, causing their corresponding factors to be only approximately commutative instead of being exactly commutative. We address this problem by introducing the concept of ε-commutativity, a relaxation of commutativity where output values are only approximately invariant under permutations of input values. Specifically, we show how ε-commutativity can be exploited for lifted model construction, downstream probabilistic inference, and prove strict bounds on the induced approximation error, thereby ensuring the practical applicability of lifted model construction while maintaining highly accurate query results. These theoretical guarantees are confirmed empirically, demonstrating comparable query accuracy at lower runtime.

</details>


### [106] [Meta$^n$: Recursive Self-Improvement through Emergent Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24735)
*Zae Myung Kim,Young-Jun Lee,Seungyeon Jwa,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对不变的元操作Ω递归其输入，Meta^n在保证稳定性的同时实现多层自我改进，实验显示其性能显著优于先前方法，关键增益源于层间的条件传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进LLM代理要么只改进答案而非解题过程，要么在元层面修改自身但为保持稳定必须保留部分编辑工具，从而将元深度限制在约二层。需要一种既能深度递归又能保证稳定性的自我改进机制。

Method: 提出Meta^n框架：定义不变的元操作Ω，反复将Ω应用于其先前产物。每层读取下层求解器栈的跟踪和生成该跟踪的代码，写出下一层的策略预处理和可调用助手库。深度由收敛决定，使用进化档案搜索层链，实现在两个骨架模型上的实验比较。

Result: 在八个基准族上，Meta^n在两个骨架上均优于先前方法。尤其在抗记忆化设计的ARC-AGI-2上，只有Meta^n得分高于零。消融实验表明大部分收益来自层与层之间传递的条件信息，并且随着深度增加出现了无提示的不同层角色。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 固定元操作并对其输入递归（Meta^n）能在不破坏系统稳定性的前提下实现任意深度的自我改进，从而优于以往的自我改进代理。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents refine answers, not the process that produces those answers. Systems that add a meta-level hold that level fixed, and those that edit themselves must leave part of their own editing machinery untouched to stay stable, capping the meta-depth they realize at roughly two. We present Meta$^n$, which keeps the meta-operation fixed and recurses on its input instead. That operation, $Ω$, is applied repeatedly to its own products, reading the traces of the solver stack below together with the code that produced them, then writing the next layer as a strategic pre-process and a library of callable helpers. Because $Ω$ never changes, it cannot destabilize the system, and because its input strictly grows, each layer reasons from a higher vantage than the last. Depth is set by convergence rather than fixed in advance, and an evolutionary archive searches over layer chains. Across two backbones, Meta$^n$ outperforms prior self-improving agents on all eight benchmark families. The sharpest case is ARC-AGI-2, built to resist skill memorization, where it alone scores above zero. Ablations indicate that most of the gain from recursion comes from the conditioning each layer passes to the next, and distinct layer roles emerge with depth although no prompt prescribes them. Code available at https://github.com/minnesotanlp/meta-n

</details>


### [107] [RACE: Scalable Statistical Estimation of Functional Consistency in LLM Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24758)
*Runyu Wang,Bo Liu,Xiaxin Zhang,Yu Han,Jiawei Cao,Xiaoye Zhang,Zhe Zhang,Yifan Yang,Peng Ping*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RACE provides a fast, forward-pass, distributional method to identify domain-consistent neurons with strong specificity and much lower compute than gradient methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To discover neurons whose functional behavior is stable across an entire domain while avoiding instance-level noise and the high computational cost of gradient-based or optimization-heavy interpretability methods.

Method: RACE is a forward-pass statistical framework that computes neuron-level domain consistency by aligning residual (activation) distributions across tokens in a domain and deriving a consistency score per neuron. It replaces gradient- or instance-level point estimates with distributional comparisons and uses perturbation experiments and token-distribution analyses for validation.

Result: RACE shows higher domain specificity than gradient-based point estimates in perturbation experiments, token-distribution-level analyses confirm selected neurons are associated with the target domain, and RACE's computational cost is about two orders of magnitude lower than gradient-based methods.

Conclusion: RACE is a promising scalable tool for domain-wide neuron consistency estimation, but further detail is needed on statistical testing, baselines, hyperparameters, and reproducibility to fully validate the claims.

Abstract: Discovering stable neuron behavior across entire domains remains a challenge in mechanistic interpretability. Existing methods often rely on instance-level point estimates or computationally expensive procedures, which either obscure population-level variability or limit scalable domain-wide analysis. We present RACE (Residual Alignment for Consistency Estimation), a forward-pass statistical framework that evaluates the domain-wide functional consistency of Transformer neurons. Perturbation experiments demonstrate that RACE achieves superior domain specificity compared to gradient-based point estimates. Meanwhile, token-distribution-level results verify the association between the selected neurons and the target domain. Furthermore, its computational overhead is two orders of magnitude lower than that of gradient-based methods.

</details>


### [108] [Evidence Blindness in Direct Corpus Interaction: Persistent Navigation with AtlasNav](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24764)
*Hongyu Guo,Zhiyu Zheng,Zhao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AtlasNav combats Evidence Blindness by building a reusable, multi-view Corpus Atlas so that queries navigate shared structure instead of rebuilding it, improving accuracy, evidence realization speed, and online cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Finite interaction budgets in direct corpus interaction cause progressive silent loss (Evidence Blindness) where required evidence is not surfaced or accessed; online-only reconstruction recovers structure too late or inefficiently.

Method: Introduce AtlasNav, a persistent multi-view corpus-navigation framework that precomputes a reusable Corpus Atlas (multi-view representations) enabling adaptive navigation per query rather than reconstructing query-conditioned structures online.

Result: On BrowseComp-Plus, AtlasNav attains 92.05% strict accuracy and cuts online inference cost by 30.21% versus prior dynamic-workspace SOTA; it realizes required evidence earlier under matched budgets, scales on PhantomWiki (10K–1M) and transfers to enterprise knowledge competitively.

Conclusion: Agentic search performance depends critically on reusable corpus structure; organizing the corpus once into a persistent Corpus Atlas yields earlier and more complete evidence realization under finite interaction budgets.

Abstract: Large language model agents are moving beyond conventional retrieval-augmented generation toward direct interaction with external corpora. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) keeps the full corpus accessible, yet reachable evidence can remain unusable under finite interaction budgets. Required evidence may fail to surface, a surfaced supporting document may remain unopened, or an opened document may fail to expose its decisive fragment. We call this progressive silent loss Evidence Blindness and quantify it through stage-wise evidence realization. Within the DCI paradigm, raw interaction adds little reusable corpus organization, while dynamic-workspace methods reconstruct a query-conditioned interaction space from each query and trajectory. In both cases, useful structure is recovered largely online. We instead formulate large-scale agentic search as finite-budget navigation over reusable corpus structure. We introduce AtlasNav, a persistent multi-view corpus-navigation framework that retains direct corpus interaction but organizes the corpus once into a Corpus Atlas, allowing each query to navigate adaptively rather than reconstruct shared structure. On BrowseComp-Plus, AtlasNav achieves 92.05% strict accuracy while reducing recorded online inference cost by 30.21% relative to the prior dynamic-workspace state of the art. Under matched budgets, it realizes the complete required evidence earlier and approaches the same model's evidence-supplied empirical reference more rapidly. The same representation principle remains effective under PhantomWiki's distinct corpus organization and controlled 10K-1M scaling, and transfers competitively to heterogeneous enterprise knowledge. These results show that agentic search depends not only on accessible evidence, but also on how the corpus is represented so that limited interaction becomes effective navigation.

</details>


### [109] [StepGuard: Learning Step-Level Guardrails with Scalable Supervision and Safety-Utility Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24777)
*Zhijie Zheng,Yu Li,Chen Qian,Yuqian Fu,Yanwei Fu,Lu Sheng,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用于工具调用前审计的步骤级守卫StepGuard，配合自动数据生成器StepGen和动态平衡训练策略Balance-GRPO，显著降低攻击成功率且保持高任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有守卫多在完整轨迹后评估，缺乏执行前对单步动作的实时审查，导致文件修改、信息泄漏、未授权操作等风险。需要一种能在步骤级别阻止危险工具调用且尽量减少误报/漏报的守卫。

Method: 提出StepGuard（步骤级守卫模型），训练数据由自动化数据引擎StepGen生成，该引擎构造在相同上下文下仅在高风险步骤上不同的安全/不安全轨迹；训练过程中引入Balance-GRPO动态平衡策略，基于观察到的安全/不安全动作准确率调整学习权重。评估在AgentDojo和AgentDyn上进行，比较无守卫与其他基线以及闭源模型。

Result: StepGuard在开源模型中取得最高平均准确率，与GPT-5.4表现相当；在AgentDojo和AgentDyn上部署时，相比无守卫场景平均攻击成功率下降77.3%，同时平均效用仅下降2.8个百分点，表明在安全性与效用间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: StepGuard在步骤级别能够在工具动作执行前进行审计，显著提升对代理攻击的防御能力并保持较高的任务效用，其性能在开源权重模型中最佳，与GPT-5.4相当。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can interact with external environments through tool invocation, but this capability also introduces security risks such as file modification, information leakage, and unauthorized actions. Existing guardrails often evaluate completed trajectories, leaving pre-execution monitoring of step-level actions underexplored. We propose StepGuard, a step-level guard model that can audit completed agent trajectories and check tool actions before they are executed. To train StepGuard, we introduce StepGen, an automatic data engine that generates safe and unsafe trajectories with the same context but different actions at the risky step. To further reduce over-defense and under-defense, we propose Balance-GRPO, which dynamically balances learning between safe and unsafe actions based on their observed accuracy. Experiments show that StepGuard achieves the highest average accuracy among open-weight guard models, with performance comparable to GPT-5.4. When used to guard agents on AgentDojo and AgentDyn, StepGuard reduces mean attack success rate by 77.3% relative to the no-guard setting, while mean utility drops by only 2.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [110] [Right Diagnoses, Decorative Reasoning:A Perturbation Audit of Medical Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24790)
*Mengzhu Xu,Jifan Gao,Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Xi Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Medical CoT texts frequently behave like post-hoc documentation rather than faithful reasoning. A 30-operator perturbation audit and the Chain-Decoupling Rate reveal widespread decoupling between displayed chains and model answers across models and datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clinicians treat CoT as evidence of medical reasoning, but faithfulness of visible reasoning is rarely tested in clinically realistic ways. Existing general-domain probes ignore clinical constraints and medical evaluations seldom inspect the chain's role, creating a need for a clinical audit.

Method: Developed a medical perturbation audit using 30 clinically motivated edit operators applied to questions and/or chains (e.g., severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation). For each perturbation they track whether the chain updates and whether the answer flips, producing a joint classification of failure modes. Evaluated 14 LLMs across four medical QA benchmarks and included clinician re-annotation (N=197) to validate edit defensibility.

Result: Three independent tests converge: panel-wide CDR=72.9% for clinically destructive edits; chain corruption often leaves accuracy unchanged; removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Clinician re-annotation found 98.5% of perturbed questions remain gold-defensible. Pattern persists across fine-tuning and model scale; closed-source models show answer-side signals consistent with decoupling. The framework and CDR are proposed as reusable metrics for audit.

Conclusion: Visible chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales in medical LLMs are often not faithful to the model’s answer: in many cases the chain does not register clinically meaningful edits while the answer also does not change, implying decoupling. The Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR) provides a quantitative measure showing large-scale decoupling.

Abstract: Clinicians read chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales as evidence of medical reasoning, but whether the visible chain plays that role is rarely tested. General-domain CoT-faithfulness probes ignore clinical cost, and medical LLM evaluations treat the chain as a black box. We close this gap with a medical perturbation audit: a 30-operator battery edits both the chain and the question with clinically motivated operators (severity reversal, negation flip, demographic swap, evidence ablation), paired with a chain-update times answer-flip joint analysis that classifies each model by its failure mode. Applied to 14 LLMs on four medical QA benchmarks, three independent tests converge: the Chain-Decoupling Rate (CDR; chain does not register the edit and the answer does not flip) is 72.9% panel-wide on clinically meaningful destructive edits, chain corruption leaves accuracy unchanged, and removing CoT prompting does not reduce accuracy. Two board-certified clinicians re-annotate N=197 perturbed questions; 98.5% leave the gold defensible. The pattern holds across medical and reasoning fine-tuning and scale; on the closed-source tier, where the chain text is unavailable, the answer-side signals are consistent with the same decoupling. Our framework and CDR provide a reusable yardstick for auditing whether medical CoT is faithful or merely documentation.

</details>


### [111] [CAFE: Self-Improving Search Agents Need Co-Evolving Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24794)
*Boyang Liu,Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Zhangyue Yin,Yibo Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Xinbing Liang,Shizheng Zhu,Yuhui Wang,Jingqi Tong,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Clive Bai,Clarenceai,Blaze Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过让代理与反馈判别器共享参数并交替训练，CAFE实现了反馈与策略的共同进化，从而在多基准和域外泛化上显著提升搜索代理表现并降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端回报无法定位中间错误或在错误积累前重定向轨迹。将纠正性反馈视为轨迹内的可学习干预，能同时让代理学会何时请求/利用反馈，且使判别器在代理改进时从结果混淆的rollout中推断有用的修正。

Method: 共享参数模型在代理-判别器两种角色间交替训练。先用基线代理失败构造的轨迹初始化基于反馈的恢复；在线RL阶段使用比较反馈估计（prompt级的call-vs-skip成功差）来调整请求回报，并通过反馈感知的优势整形在反馈前后重加权token优势；离线阶段用匹配的成功/失败轨迹做rollout派生的偏好优化以学习反馈。

Result: 在7个agentic搜索基准上，CAFE平均优于评估的RL搜索代理，并在6个域外测试集上保持收益，减少答案层面的幻觉。一侧消融显示仅改进代理或仅改进判别器会最终陷入平台期，而交替两者更新能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: CAFE将搜索代理与反馈判别器耦合为共享参数的模型，通过在轨迹内作为干预学习何时请求并如何使用纠正反馈，使反馈随策略共同进化，从而提高检索/生成性能并减少幻觉；实验证明在多基准和域外泛化上均优于对比的RL基线，且交替更新代理与判别器是持续改进的关键。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised search agents learn when and how to retrieve evidence, but terminal rewards neither localize intermediate errors nor redirect an ongoing trajectory before those errors compound. Treating corrective feedback as a learned in-trajectory intervention couples the two roles: the agent must decide when to request and use feedback, while the critic must infer useful corrections from outcome-confounded rollouts whose failure patterns shift as the agent improves. We introduce CAFE (Coupled Agent--Feedback Evolution), a framework in which a shared-parameter model alternates between search-agent and critic roles. CAFE initializes feedback-conditioned recovery from trajectories built around the base agent's own failures, then couples online and offline optimization. During online RL, a comparative feedback estimate uses a prompt-level call--skip success gap to shape request returns, while feedback-aware advantage shaping reweights token advantages before and after feedback. Offline, rollout-derived preference optimization learns feedback from matched successful and unsuccessful trajectories. On seven agentic search benchmarks, CAFE outperforms the evaluated RL-based search agents on average, retains its gains across all six out-of-domain benchmarks, and reduces answer-level hallucinations. One-sided ablations show that improving only the agent or only the critic eventually plateaus, whereas alternating the two updates continues to improve performance. These findings suggest that a self-improving search agent needs feedback that co-evolves with the policy it guides.

</details>


### [112] [Strictly Causal Streaming Video Anomaly Detection with a Theoretically-Grounded State-Space Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24810)
*Yogesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个严格因果、O(1)更新的流式SSM异常检测器，理论连接衰减谱与延迟，实测在M3 Pro上延迟极低但准确率仍落后于非因果基线，门控机制主导响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SSM的视频异常检测在内部仍依赖片段缓冲或窗口、缺乏关于时间记忆与检测延迟关系的理论解释，并且只用GPU吞吐评估效率而非目标的边缘硬件——需要一个真正流式、低延迟、面向边缘设备的因果方法并给出延迟理论分析。

Method: 提出一个对角线性状态空间递归（diagonal linear SSM），带有输入与状态依赖的衰减门（decay gate）；使用冻结的视觉骨干提取嵌入，采用因果的下一步嵌入预测进行自监督训练；无lookahead和窗口缓冲，固定大小状态，逐帧更新为常数复杂度；理论上导出衰减谱与检测延迟/可检测最短异常长度的闭式关系。

Result: 在UCSD Ped2和CUHK Avenue上验证：从学习到的基础衰减预测的settling延迟界（57–59帧）远大于实测检测延迟（Ped2:1.6帧，Avenue:18.4帧），表明门控而非基础衰减主导响应；在Apple M3 Pro上测得端到端延迟约0.74–0.77 ms/帧（>1300 FPS）；未调优时在Ped2和Avenue的帧级AUC分别为67.9%和70.2%，低于非因果SSM基线。

Conclusion: 事件边界门控（event boundary gate）而非基础衰减谱主导检测器的响应性；提出的严格因果流式SSM在每帧O(1)时间/内存下更新状态，实现极低端到端延迟与高吞吐，但在准确率上落后于先前的非因果SSM基线。

Abstract: Recent work has applied Mamba style state space models (SSMs) to video anomaly detection, yet existing approaches still rely on buffering clips or windows internally, lack a theoretical account of how temporal memory relates to detection latency, and benchmark efficiency only through GPU throughput rather than the edge hardware these methods are intended to target. We introduce a strictly causal streaming anomaly detector whose fixed size state is updated in O(1) time and memory per incoming frame, with no lookahead and no clip buffering. Its temporal core is a diagonal linear state space recurrence with an input and state dependent decay gate, trained self supervised through causal next embedding prediction on a frozen visual backbone. We derive a closed form relationship between the recurrence decay spectrum and both detection delay and the shortest anomaly it can reliably capture, then validate empirically on UCSD Ped2 and CUHK Avenue. The settling delay bound predicted from the learned base decay (57 to 59 frames) sits far above the measured detection delay (1.6 and 18.4 frames), showing that the event boundary gate, not the base decay, governs responsiveness. We further report end to end latency and throughput measured directly on Apple M3 Pro hardware, 0.74 ms and 0.77 ms per frame (over 1300 FPS), rather than simulated GPU numbers. With an untuned initial configuration the method reaches 67.9 percent and 70.2 percent frame level AUC on Ped2 and Avenue, trailing prior non causal SSM baselines in accuracy. Ablations over decay rate, state size, and gating reveal that the gate contribution is dataset size dependent, hurting accuracy on the smaller Ped2 training set but helping on the larger Avenue one. Closing this accuracy gap and extending evaluation to a third, larger benchmark are immediate next steps.

</details>


### [113] [Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge for LLM-Based Knowledge Graph QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24824)
*Emanuel Kitzelmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CES-PK，使用三值约束语义对LLM候选答案进行符号化验证，在Hetionet上验证后提高了精确率且不损失召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖脆弱的完整语义解析（如生成SPARQL），要么纯依赖LLM推理没有符号保证。目标是兼顾鲁棒性与形式化证据，通过轻量约束提供对候选答案的符号支持与剔除无效答案。

Method: 提出CES-PK问题形式化：在不构建可执行逻辑形式的情况下，用类型、关系和排除等符号约束对LLM生成的候选实体进行验证；引入三值语义以处理KG的不完全性；在Hetionet生物医学KG上进行实现与评估。

Result: 实验证明：过滤被违反约束的候选项能提高精确率，而对于未知约束的候选则保留以避免因KG不完整造成的误拒，从而保持召回率；满足的约束还能作为正向符号证据用于对剩余候选项排序。

Conclusion: 通过对LLM生成候选答案施加源自问题的轻量级符号约束，并采用三值约束语义（满足、违反、未知），能够在开放世界知识图上过滤不合法答案、保留不确定答案，从而在提升精确率的同时保持召回率。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for knowledge graph question answering (KGQA), but can fail to correctly ground answers in the underlying graph. Current approaches to LLM-based KGQA either rely on full semantic parsing into executable queries such as SPARQL, which is brittle in practice due to complex schemas or incompleteness of real-world KGs, or on LLM-reasoning and answer generation over KGs, which can be more robust but lacks formal guarantees. In this work, we study a complementary setting in which \emph{candidate} answers are generated by an LLM-based system and subsequently verified using lightweight symbolic constraints derived from the question. We introduce \emph{Constrained Entity Selection under Partial Knowledge (CES-PK)}, a problem formulation that focuses on eliminating invalid answers and providing symbolic support for valid ones without requiring construction of executable logical forms. To account for incomplete KGs, we employ a three-valued constraint semantics (\emph{satisfied, violated, unknown}) that avoids incorrect rejections under open-world assumptions. To demonstrate the effects of our method, we instantiate this framework over the Hetionet biomedical knowledge graph and evaluate the impact of type, relation, and exclusion constraints. Experiments show that precision improves by filtering invalid candidates, while recall is preserved due to retaining candidates whose constraints are not explicitly violated. Satisfied constraints provide additional positive symbolic evidence to rank remaining candidates.

</details>


### [114] [A Dual-Dimensional LLM Framework for Automated Item Incidental Content Similarity Analysis in Large-Scale Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24825)
*Jing Huang,Jihong Zhang,Hua-Hua Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用LLM驱动的结构化分解+语义相关性双维相似性框架，可更有效检测并缓解试题内容冗余，改进试题库管理与自适应选题表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模评测和自动题目生成的普及，题干措辞或情境无意重复引发构念无关冗余，传统BLEU或余弦相似度难以同时捕捉语义与结构层面的细微差异，需更具认知与语义感知力的方法来自动化检测与缓解冗余。

Method: 提出一个双维框架，将试题相似性分解为“结构化分解”（Structured Decomposition）和“语义相关性”（Semantic Relatedness）；利用大型语言模型计算并整合这两类度量；通过心理测量学验证（如局部依赖指标、试题参数聚类）和CAT仿真比较LLM度量与传统度量的性能。

Result: LLM衍生的相似性度量与构念无关的局部依赖指标更相关，能形成更连贯的参数分组；在CAT仿真中，纳入LLM相似性约束可在微小效率损失下显著提高估计稳定性并降低偏差，优于基于传统文本度量的约束。

Conclusion: 基于大型语言模型的双维自动试题相似性分析框架（AISA）在识别构念无关的局部依赖、生成更一致的试题参数聚类以及在计算机自适应测试（CAT）中通过相似性约束提升估计稳健性和降低偏差方面，优于传统文本相似度指标。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [115] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedV-KGQA通过本地图谱增补、知识图嵌入和主题实体锚定，实现了在实体共享但关系垂直分区的多机构环境下的无中心化多跳KGQA，性能接近集中式方案并具备一定鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界KGQA数据因治理和数据主权分散在不同机构，集中式系统无法回答当事实跨垂直分区分散时的多跳问答；需要一种在不共享原始三元组或关系参数的前提下实现跨孤岛多跳推理的方案。

Method: 提出FedV-KGQA框架：在垂直分区的知识图中，组织共享实体但拥有不同的关系集合。方法包括本地图谱增补(local graph enrichment)、基于知识图嵌入的表征学习以保证原始三元组和关系参数不离开各自数据孤岛，以及主题实体锚定(topic entity anchoring)机制用于在无运行时跨孤岛通信的情况下将问题定位到正确的图邻域。

Result: 在三个基准上评估了12种模型配置，FedV-KGQA表现接近集中式系统，能够推广至3跳推理，并对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持数据边界和不进行运行时跨孤岛通信的前提下，实现了有效的多跳KGQA；但对实体对齐、关系重叠场景、更深跳数和隐私泄露分析等方面仍需进一步验证和扩展。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [116] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SPO++：通过 action-token 测度的终端优势标准化和按策略事件组织证据，修正 SPO 的衡量不匹配，提升了样本效率，且该归一化是性能改进的主要因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Group-relative RL 在需要等待同一提示的兄弟 rollout 时在长且可变的工具使用轨迹上代价高昂；SPO 通过持久的提示级价值估计移除了这一依赖，但在优势中心化与演员损失的度量不匹配上存在问题。

Method: 识别并修正 SPO 的‘轨迹白化’与演员消耗的 token 加权量不一致的问题：对终端结果优势在 action-token 测度下进行标准化；同时按生成策略的事件（policy event）而非学习者接收顺序组织提示证据。

Result: 在 ALFWorld（两种模型规模）和 Math-TIR 上的配对运行中，SPO++ 比 SPO 在在线学习效率上有提升；消融实验显示按 action-token 测度的归一化是贡献最大的成分。

Conclusion: SPO++ 修正了 SPO 在将轨迹中心化与行为空间的 token 加权量之间的不匹配，通过对终端结果优势按 action-token 测度标准化并重新组织提示证据，显著提升了在线学习效率。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


### [117] [Recursive Experiential-Working Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24876)
*Zhaochen Yu,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Kaiyuan Chen,Zhe Zhao,Mengdi Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Recuris通过耦合工作记忆与体验记忆并用元代理进行验证门控的局部更新，构建了一个有界的递归记忆演化机制，显著提升了长时域任务中的技能调用与总体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期任务中，增长的历史会掩盖当前任务状态并导致技能调用不对齐，阻碍递归自我改进（RSI）；需要一种将经验与当前需要耦合并局部化失败以实现可控的记忆更新与递归演化。

Method: 提出Recuris：一种递归的Experiential-Working Memory架构。工作记忆（Working Memory）跟踪任务进度并指导从体验记忆（Experiential Memory）中选择技能，使技能调用基于当前需要而非完整历史；执行过程被作为结构化证据，以将失败定位到具体记忆组件；一个固定的元代理（Meta-Agent）将证据转化为受验证门控的技能记忆（Skill Memory）局部更新，从而形成有界的递归记忆演化循环。

Result: 在四个长时域基准和十种模型上，Recuris在37个已完成的模型-基准对中有35个提高了任务成功率；例如在tau-bench上对GPT-5.6 Sol提高+17.8点、对Claude Opus 5提高+15.6点（Opus 5达87.9%），在SkillFlow上对Qwen3.6-27B/35B分别提高+16.6/+13.5点；在最长任务上优势扩大到+32.2点，常见长时失败下降最多达80%。代码已公开（https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Recuris）。

Conclusion: 递归演化的记忆（bounded recursive memory evolution）是实现可扩展递归自我改进的有前景基础，使代理能将累积经验持续转化为更有效的长时行为。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) remains hard in long-horizon tasks, where growing histories obscure the task state and misalign skill invocation. We introduce Recuris, a recursive Experiential-Working Memory architecture for long-horizon agent harnesses, in which Working Memory tracks task progress and guides skill selection from Experiential Memory, grounding skill use in current needs rather than the full history. This coupling also turns execution into structured evidence that localizes failures to specific memory components. Across tasks, a fixed Meta-Agent turns that evidence into localized, validation-gated updates to Skill Memory that reshape execution and yield new evidence, forming a bounded recursive memory-evolution loop. Across four long-horizon benchmarks and ten models, Recuris improves task success in 35 of the 37 completed model-benchmark pairs, carrying frontier models to SOTA-level task success: on tau-bench it adds +17.8 points to GPT-5.6 Sol and +15.6 to Claude Opus 5, taking Opus 5 to 87.9%, and +16.6/+13.5 points on Qwen3.6-27B/35B on SkillFlow. The advantage widens as the interaction horizon grows, to +32.2 points on the longest tasks, and common long-horizon failures fall by up to 80%. These results position recursively evolving memory as a scalable foundation for RSI, enabling agents to continuously transform accumulated experience into increasingly effective long-horizon behavior. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Recuris

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Decomposing Browser Pipeline Architectures for DOM-Sourced Particle Effects: Worker Offload, WebGL, and WebAssembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23609)
*Hossein Asadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Layer-aware, reproducible measurements show that micro-level wins (WASM, workers, renderer choice) only matter when they fix the dominant bottleneck; otherwise they may not improve user-visible FPS.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Teams often apply Web Workers, WebGL, and WebAssembly interchangeably to “make it faster” without knowing which browser layer is the bottleneck. The work aims to identify when layer-local wins produce end-to-end improvements.

Method: Controlled architectural decomposition using DOM-sourced particle pipelines (P0–P5) that isolate thread placement, renderer choice, and simulation backend; a reproducible harness measuring interactive pacing, high-load end-to-end stress, and a simulation-only microbenchmark; cross-browser and cross-host replication (Chrome/Firefox, Intel/NVIDIA, Colab Tesla T4).

Result: Four main findings: (1) Worker offload substantially improves interactive pacing on the primary Chrome host. (2) AssemblyScript accelerates the Chrome update kernel (~1.5–1.85× in sim-only). (3) WASM sim-only improvements do not always increase product FPS under heavy WebGL load (~250k particles): P5≤P4 on some hosts, indicating other bottlenecks. (4) Renderer ranking and absolute margins depend on browser/GPU/host configuration. The contribution is a bottleneck-aware measurement methodology.

Conclusion: Optimizing a single layer (e.g., using WebAssembly or offloading to workers) does not guarantee end-to-end FPS/UX gains; only when the optimized layer is the dominant bottleneck will microbenchmark wins propagate to user-visible performance.

Abstract: Local optimization does not necessarily yield end-to-end optimization in layered browser architectures. Teams often treat Web Workers, WebGL, and WebAssembly as interchangeable ways to "make it faster," yet each lever targets a different layer. We present a controlled architectural decomposition--using DOM-sourced particle pipelines as a concrete workload--of five particle pipelines (P1-P5), with an additional non-particle CSS-layer baseline (P0), that isolates thread placement, renderer choice, and simulation backend. Using a reproducible harness we measure interactive pacing, high-load end-to-end stress, and a simulation-only microbenchmark, plus same-host cross-browser / dual-GPU-class replication (Firefox 153+Intel UHD; Chrome 138+NVIDIA NVK) and an independent second-host slice on Google Colab (Chrome 150, Tesla T4, n=5). Four results stand out. (1) Worker offload improves interactive pacing on paper-primary Chrome (approx. 144 vs approx. 52 FPS; Cliff's delta=1, n=10). (2) AssemblyScript speeds the Chrome update kernel by about 1.5-1.6x on the primary host and approx. 1.85x on Colab T4 (sim-only). (3) Under approx. 250k WebGL particles the Chrome sim-only WASM win need not raise product FPS (P5<=P4 on primary/Firefox; P5 approx. P4 on Colab T4); in-worker CPU phase timers show simulate still dominates the accounted budget, so the non-translation is not a simple "draw dominates CPU" story. (4) Renderer ranking and absolute margins vary across browser/GPU/host configurations. The contribution is bottleneck-aware architectural measurement: identify the dominant layer and test whether a layer win propagates to user-visible FPS.

</details>


### [119] [From Traceability to Justifiability: Accountability Structures in Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23610)
*Rashid Azarang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Most AI delivery platforms' default records do not bind evaluated models to deployed behavior via content-addressed identities; attestation tooling often insufficiently published for verification; a minority of adopters do realize end-to-end bindings


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Determine whether published records from AI delivery pipelines actually claim and substantiate that the evaluated artifact is the artifact deployed, using public materials only

Method: Two-part empirical audit of deployment provenance and attestation

Result: No platform published content-addressed behavioral identity; immutable nominal versioning common; many attestations publish source-only releases preventing verification of declared bindings; where checkable most adopters realized end-to-end binding except identity issues

Conclusion: Field-level documentation and attestation records are structurally insufficient for justifying transitions from evaluation to deployment; specific falsifiable conditions and an expiry clock are provided

Abstract: A pipeline promoting an AI system publishes records claiming the thing evaluated is the thing deployed and that the evidence licensed the transition. We measure, from public material only, whether those records can express that claim and whether it holds where declared. First, a two-class documentation survey of 47 delivery platforms (20 CI/CD, 27 model-serving/agent) under one fixed three-label protocol, graded twice (second pass blind), every consulted page pinned by content hash and date. Across 188 double-graded cells we found no platform whose default record emits a content-addressed identity of the behavioral tuple (model version, instructions, tool definitions, retrieval and runtime configuration); the blind pass grades that column default on zero of 47. Immutable nominal versioning is meanwhile arriving as the agent platforms' default answer (16 of 27): version integers behind mutable pointers, a layer the artifact supply chain already found insufficient. Second, an instrument computes realized assurance depth from a pipeline's published exhaust alone and compares it with the declared depth. Applied to a frozen two-stratum frame of 30 public repositories graded twice from a hashed archive (second pass blind; cell-level agreement 23 and 19 of 30, both passes independently finding the same five full realizations), the sharpest result is a verifiability hole: seven of the 15 repositories chosen for adopting attestation tooling publish source-only releases, so the binding their workflows declare cannot be checked where declared. Where checkable it mostly checks out: five of seven measurable adopters realize the binding end to end; both shortfalls fall at identity binding. Together the results locate the field's records structurally short of justifiability, the one rung that can refuse a transition. The survey carries an expiry clock; we state what would falsify each finding.

</details>


### [120] [REFINE: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach for Evidence-Guided Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23611)
*Muhammad Waseem,Aakash Ahmad,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: REFINE is an evidence-aware multi-agent pipeline for LLM-based Java refactoring that reduces code smells substantially but still requires compilation/testing/human review due to residual risks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper addresses the problem of ensuring automated refactorings by LLMs reduce code smells without introducing new issues or changing behavior-critical structures. It motivates a tool-agnostic, evidence-aware multi-agent pipeline to systematically identify smells, plan refactorings, apply LLM-based transformations, and verify preservation.

Method: REFINE integrates static-analysis-guided smell identification, smell-informed planning, LLM transformation across multiple agents, automated re-analysis and preservation checks, and structured reporting; evaluated on 450 files across 15 projects with three LLMs and compared to a direct-prompt baseline.

Result: REFINE achieves substantial reductions in detected code smells (around 68–73%) across three LLM configurations and outperforms a direct-prompt baseline on median smell reduction, edit size, and fewer public-method removals; however, overall quality improvements are mixed and some preservation risks remain.

Conclusion: REFINE is effective at reducing code smells and produces better candidates than direct prompting, but outputs remain proposals needing further checks; future work should address inconsistent broader quality gains and residual preservation risks.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new opportunities for automated code refactoring. However, generated changes must reduce targeted quality problems without introducing new issues or altering behaviour-relevant code structures. We introduce REFINE (Refactoring with Evidence-aware Flow for Integrated ageNtic Execution), a tool-agnostic, evidence-aware multi-agent approach for generating Java file-level refactoring candidates. REFINE combines static-analysis-guided smell identification, smell-informed planning, LLM-based transformation, automated re-analysis, preservation checks, and structured reporting.
  We evaluate REFINE on 450 Java files from 15 open-source systems, producing 1,350 model-pass outputs using OpenAI GPT-5.5, Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview, and Anthropic Claude Opus 4.8. REFINE reduces detected code smells by 68.26%, 72.79%, and 68.49% across the three configurations, respectively, with the strongest reductions observed for major smells. A matched 150-file direct-prompt baseline shows that REFINE achieves a higher median code-smell reduction with smaller edits and fewer public-method removals. However, broader quality improvements are inconsistent, and preservation checks reveal residual risks, including assert/fail-call changes and public-method removal. Therefore, REFINE outputs should be treated as refactoring candidates requiring compilation, testing, dependency analysis, and human review before adoption in repository- or system-level settings.

</details>


### [121] [SDR Driver for Precise Timing Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23614)
*Fabrizio Pollastri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 为HackRF One扩展了时序功能并将实现重构为SoapySDR驱动，使用AI辅助生成代码，显著降低了开发成本并提升了可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使廉价的HackRF One具备用于精确时序应用（需要同步采样与可控时基）的能力，降低实现成本并提升软件可维护性。

Method: 在原有固件基础上扩展时序相关功能（同步采样、时间尺度控制、时钟频率调整）；最初用Python包装实现概念验证，随后重构为SoapySDR兼容驱动；采用AI辅助生成新驱动代码并进行有限调试以投入运行。

Result: 新驱动在较少调试下即可运行，开发工作量降低了一个数量级以上，同时代码维护性得到改善；实现了同步采样、时间尺度控制与时钟频率调节等关键功能。

Conclusion: 通过AI辅助的开发流程，研究人员成功为低成本HackRF One实现了精确时序功能的驱动，证明可在较短时间内获得可运行的SoapySDR兼容驱动并提高可维护性。

Abstract: This paper describes the development of software drivers that enable precise timing applications with the low-cost HackRF One software-defined radio (SDR). The original firmware was extended with timing functions for synchronized sampling, time-scale control, and clock-frequency adjustment. An initial implementation based on a Python wrapper was later redesigned as a SoapySDR-compatible driver. Using an AI-assisted development workflow, the new driver was generated from the existing HackRF One and SoapySDR codebases and became operational after only limited debugging. The approach reduced development effort by more than an order of magnitude while improving software maintainability.

</details>
